## AD-2

Acoustic fixed-chain processor; partial, checked 2026-10-18. Included for distinct resonance, ambience and feedback processing, not its preset/mode count. [Manufacturer](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ad-2/).

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->

| Field | Finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression | No built-in pedal/toe or external EXP socket in published connector inventory. |
| K/F/B | **3/1/0**, manufacturer text controls; visual audit pending. |
| Display | CHECK/battery indicator; screen diagonal/color/touch not specified. |
| Processing / categories | Acoustic Resonance, Ambience reverb and notch filtering; fixed controls, not a selectable multi-model library. Numeric simultaneous-block count unpublished. |
| Memory | Factory/user/total preset memory unverified. |
| Synth / acoustic | Acoustic-electric resonance restoration, optimized for piezo pickups. [Roland distinguishes AD-2 from AC-3](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404893445403): AD-2 restores acoustic input tone; AC-3 converts electric guitar tone. Do not classify AD-2 as an electric-to-acoustic simulator. Synth remains unverified. |
| Dimensions / mass | **73W×129D×59H mm;420g including battery**. |
| I/O | INPUT,OUTPUT ¼-inch; balanced LINE OUT ¼-inch TRS;DC IN. USB/MIDI absent from published inventory. |
| Power | 9V alkaline6LR61 or optional PSA;65mA;approximately5h battery,condition-dependent. PSA-family voltage/polarity cross-check below; USB power unverified. |
| Editor / platforms | Editor,drivers and software platforms unverified. |
| IR / NAM / capture | Unverified. |
| Practical features | Buffered bypass,mute;LINE OUT can omit Ambience independently. Looper/drums/scenes unverified. |
| IDR price | [Indexed Shopee offer](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-AD-2-AD2-Acoustic-Preamp-Guitar-Acoustic-Effect-i.16879370.651392518): **Rp2,150,500**,Seven Stereo Music & Audio Store,Bandung;seller16879370/item651392518. Checked2026-10-18;index crawled roughly2months earlier,delivery dates19–22August. New/used condition,live stock,bundle,warranty andcheckout voucher unverified;late-delivery compensation isnot an immediate price discount. No qualified used ask. |
| I/O / routing and mute | [Manufacturer Portuguese manual,revision02,©2016](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/AD-2_M_ptb02_W.pdf),translated text,checked2026-10-18: holdfootswitch≥2s tomute;CHECKflashes,pressagain tounmute. INPUT insertion powersunit;unplug whenunused. LINE OUT isbalancedmono,explicitly notheadphones. Holdpedal duringpower-on,adjustAMBIENCE,release tofinish: reverb canfeedbothoutputs orOUTPUTonly. Exactknobpositions/pinout needvisualverification. |
| Power / source priority | Same manual: PSAadapter takespriority overinstalledbattery;retainingbattery allowscontinuedoperation ifadapter disconnects. This source-priority passage alone does not establish USB power or DC plug polarity; see the separate PSA-family cross-check below. |
| Acoustic / pickup compatibility | [Exact-model Roland support](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404885412763), updated 2025-01-24: non-piezo pickups may yield a weaker effect. [Simulator-input qualification](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404885409691): feeding AC-3 or similar simulated acoustic output may not achieve the intended result; guitar, pickup and tone settings can make processing too weak or excessive. Translated Japanese manufacturer text, checked 2026-10-18. |
| I/O / output voicing | [Roland output FAQ](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404885409179), updated 2025-01-24: OUTPUT is voiced primarily for acoustic guitar amplifiers and carries Ambience, Notch and Acoustic Resonance. LINE OUT targets PA/stage amplification and can omit Ambience for mixer-side reverb; these are not simply two identical sockets. |
| Power / named adapter cross-check | AD-2 explicitly specifies optional PSA-series power. [Roland PSA-S specifications](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201953449-BOSS-PSA-S-Adaptor-Specifications), updated2026-06-16, specify **regulated9V DC,0.5A,center-negative**, with100–240V50/60Hz mains input. Family-member cross-check, not inspection of the actual regional/replacement supply or connector diameter. **500mA is adapter capacity;65mA is AD-2's published draw.** Confirm regional plug compatibility. |
| Audio / electrical specifications | [AD-2 own specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ad-2/): nominal input and both outputs **−20dBu**; input **10MΩ**. OUTPUT impedance **1kΩ**,recommended load≥10kΩ; LINE OUT impedance **600Ω**,recommended load≥600Ω. Output impedance and connected load are separate ratings; no measured results claimed. |

### AD-2 — expression screening and published specification conflict

Checked2026-10-28 against the [original English e01 manual](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/AD-2_e01_W.pdf),©2016,and [current product specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ad-2/). Published connector inventory has no analog EXP;LINE OUT explicitly cannot serve as headphones. Single pedal switch operates effects/mute,not documented preset down/up. Acoustic-electric input is documented;ordinary electric-to-acoustic simulation is not established. 73W×129Dmm fits preference,without meeting direct-expression requirement.

**Revision/source conflict retained:**e01 specifies55mA,approximately6h alkaline life and450g including battery;current product lists65mA,approximately5h and420g including battery. Do not average these or infer which revision a seller supplies. Direct analog volume placement/range/recall are inapplicable;tail/loop attenuation remains unverified. Existing buffered-bypass andmute qualifications remain.

## AD-8

Acoustic-guitar multi-effects: body modeling, EQ, reverb and feedback suppression; distinct from AD-10 and single-function acoustic simulators. Checked 2026-10-17; partial. No documented bass/vocal membership adopted.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->

| Field | Verified finding / limitation |
|---|---|
| Expression | No built-in expression pedal or external EXP socket in the [official manual inventory][AD8-M]; no toe switch listed. |
| Controls | **10 rotary / 3 footswitches / 4 buttons**, counted from [BOSS Japan’s control list][AD8-J]. Stereo/mono and ground-lift switches are separate. Text-derived inventory, not visual inspection. |
| Display | **Seven-segment LED**; published diagonal, emission color and touch specification unverified. [AD8-S] |
| Processing / category choices | **Six acoustic body models**, four-band EQ, acoustic reverb and automatic anti-feedback. Explicit maximum simultaneous count remains unverified; the manual’s reverb choices are documented below. [AD8-S] |
| Factory / user memory | **Four stored setups plus live Manual mode**, not five stored presets. [AD8-J] Manual pp20/22 identifies four factory-filled, rewritable memories for strumming, fingerstyle, flat-pick playing and blues. Reset overwrites all four; no extra factory bank. [AD8-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | Piezo-acoustic body/microphone modeling, not proof of electric-to-acoustic conversion. Synth engine unverified. [AD8-S] |
| Dimensions / mass | **250 W × 180 D × 60 H mm; 1.9 kg including batteries**, manual pp25–26. [AD8-M] **Conflict:** Japan lists **250 × 190 × 75 mm**, same mass. [AD8-J] Large under either published width; reason for regional difference unestablished. |
| I/O | Quarter-inch INPUT; quarter-inch stereo TRS PHONES; quarter-inch electric-guitar-amp output; unbalanced quarter-inch A/mono and B; balanced XLR A/B; PSA DC input. XLRs are outputs, not microphone inputs. [AD8-S] |
| I/O / impedance conflict | Manual p25 gives **4.7 MΩ input**, regional web specification **20 MΩ**. Preserve both; no revision explanation verified. [AD8-M], [AD8-S] |
| Power / batteries / USB | **9 V DC, 120 mA in manual/current regional pages; six AA or PSA adapter**. Historical170mA and indexed2011 200mA conflicts documented below. Manual rates carbon batteries about **9 h**, alkaline **23 h**, conditions dependent. Polarity unresolved. No USB connector in the complete port list; USB-powered operation not supported by that inventory. [AD8-M], [AD8-S] |
| Editor / USB | No full editor platform established; no USB or MIDI connector listed. [AD8-S] |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR, native/converted NAM and proprietary capture loading unverified; COSM body models do not establish user imports. |
| Looper / drums / live | Tuner with mute, reference **438–445 Hz**, accuracy ±3 cents; balanced stereo DI with ground lift. Looper/drums unverified. [AD8-S] |

### AD-8 — manual processing and stage behavior, 2026-10-17

[Own ©2003 manual][AD8-M], printed pp9,13–20,22; text inspection only.

| Field | Verified behavior / limit |
|---|---|
| Processing / reverb and EQ | **Three reverb alternatives: Ambience, Room, Hall**, plus off; not three simultaneous reverbs. Midrange frequency adjusts **200 Hz–1.6 kHz**. |
| Looper / routing / startup | Power-up selects Manual with effects on. Mono output sends identical A/B signals. The output-level knob affects processed sound only; bypass/mute uses its fixed output state. The chosen bypass/mute behavior survives power-off. |
| Live / anti-feedback | One feedback point is detected; attenuation also covers its octave above. Press both memory pedals to detect, hold both for at least two seconds to exit. Settings can be written with a memory, but are not saved in Manual mode. Anti-feedback starts off after power-up. |

### AD-8 — body pairs and historical specification conflict, 2026-10-17

| Field | Verified evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / acoustic model pairs | [Own manual, printed p10][AD8-M]: **1 D-28/U87; 2 000-28/U67; 3 J-45/U67; 4 B-25/U87; 5 D-40/C12; 6 Jose Ramirez/C12**. Guitar makers respectively Martin, Martin, Gibson, Gibson, Guild and Jose Ramirez; microphones Neumann for 1–4, AKG for 5–6. These are six fixed modeled combinations, not six guitars times three independently selectable microphones, physical mic inputs or user captures. |
| Power / historical draw conflict | [Manufacturer 2008 catalogue, indexed AD-8 specifications](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/boss_catalog_2008.pdf) lists **170 mA at 9 V maximum**, alongside20 MΩ input and250 ×180 ×60 mm. This conflicts with the own manual’s **120 mA**, also corroborated by [2012 catalogue](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/boss_catalog_2012.pdf) and [2014 catalogue](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/boss_catalog_2014.pdf). No documented revision or correction reconciles the figures. Do not treat170 mA as an adapter rating. Full2008 PDF exceeded reader size limit; evidence is its indexed model-specific text, not visual inspection. |


### AD-8 — analog levels and power switching

Checked2026-10-18 against [own manual,printed pp13,25–26][AD8-M] and [regional specifications](https://www.boss.info/se/products/ad-8/specifications/). No device operation ordownloads.

| Field | Finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / nominal levels and impedances | **Nominal input/output−20dBu**;unbalanced/electric-amp outputs **1kΩ**,balancedXLR **600Ω**,phones **33Ω**. Regional recommended load **10kΩ orgreater**;do not reinterpret this as a minimum headphone rating. Nominal levels are not maximum headroom orheadphone power. Manual’s Ω-valued output lines are mislabeled NominalOutputLevel;regional page identifies them as impedances. Existing4.7MΩ/20MΩ input conflict remains. |
| Power / input-jack switching and bundle | Inserting the INPUT plug switches the uniton;removing it switches off. Manufacturer accessories list **sixR6AA batteries**,with **PSA adapter optional**. These are original package contents,not proof ofwhat seller item9817836384 includes. NoUSB/battery-runtime change inferred. |
| I/O / ground lift | NOR/LIFT disconnects **pin1 ground** from inputground whenLIFT isselected. This is an output ground-loop control,not a phantom-power ormicrophone-input switch. Default recommendation isNOR. |

| Field | Source evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Power / 2011 catalogue conflict | [Roland’s 2011 catalogue, indexed AD SERIES/AD-8 specification](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/boss_catalog_2011.pdf) lists **200mA**. Full PDF open failed; this is **model-specific indexed text only**, not a visually inspected specification. Preserve alongside **120mA** in the own manual/current regional specifications and **170mA** in the2008 catalogue. Revision or correction unverified; do not average the figures or infer a changed battery runtime. |

The manual’s example saying400Hz conflicts with its explicit range/default; do not expand the supported range from that example. [Own manual pp18,20][AD8-M] |
| Display / memory indicators | Own manualp17 offers one lit selected-memory indicator or a cumulative indicator count. Holding SELECT≥2seconds switches patterns; setting survives power-off. This is LED indication behavior, not screen diagonal/color/touch evidence. [Own manual p17][AD8-M] |
### AD-8 — direct-expression screening (2026-10-28)

[Own manual][AD8-M], printed pp13–14, confirms the complete rear connector inventory has no EXP socket: direct analog expression-volume requirement fails. MEMORY down/up cycles Manual and four memories in opposite directions; PHONES is dedicated. Existing regional footprint conflict remains; both widths exceed preference. Normal electric-guitar tonal suitability remains unverified. Direct-pedal position/range is inapplicable to this inventory; hand OUTPUT LEVEL does not establish pedal assignment or universal attenuation during bypass. No device testing.

## AD-10

Acoustic-electric guitar multi-effects/preamp, checked2026-10-17; partial. [Manufacturer product/specifications][AD10-P] explicitly document multiple effect families; not the dedicated BOSS AC-3 acoustic simulator. Bass/vocal capability and ownership unestablished.

| Field | Verified evidence / gaps |
|---|---|
| Expression | External EV-30,FV-500H/L,EV-5 supported through shared EXP/CTL1,2; alternatively two footswitches. Built-in/toe unverified. |
| Controls | **14 rotary /3 footswitches /19 named buttons**, text-derived inventory below;power separate. Visual corroboration pending. |
| Display | **2-digit7-segment LED**;diagonal/color/touch unverified. |
| Processing / choices | Resonance3 (Natural/Wide/Bright),compressor2 (Soft/Hard),chorus3 (Normal/Mild/Bright),delay3 (Delay/Modulation/Reverse),ambience3 (Short/Mid/Long);off excluded from counts. Four-bandEQ,lowcut,boost,feedback reduction+two notches. Aggregate simultaneous count unestablished. |
| Factory / user memory | **10 stored setups**,plusManual mode;factory-filled count unknown. |
| Synth / acoustic | Acoustic Resonance restores pickup/body response;electric-to-acoustic conversion and synth unestablished. |
| Dimensions / mass | **217W×161D×65Hmm;1.3kg including batteries**. |
| I/O | Two quarter-inch guitar inputs,send/return,stereoXLRouts,quarter-inchL/phones+R/mono,TRSEXP/CTL,USB-B,DC. |
| Power / batteries | **300mA draw;6AA alkaline,about6h**;included adapter. **9VDC** confirmed through model-specific PSA-100 compatibility below;polarity/USB-power unresolved. |
| IR / NAM / captures | User loading unestablished. |
| Editor / platforms | USB audio drivers listed forWindows10/11 andmacOS14+;full editor unverified. |
| Looper / drums / routing | **80s looper;2-in/2-out USB audio**. Outputs selectable with/without effects or separated input channels. Drums/scenes unknown. |

### AD-10 — manual routing and memory qualifications

Checked2026-10-17 against [official English manual][AD10-PDF], file eng04_W,14pages, ©2017 Roland; printed pp2–3,5,9–13.

| Field | Verified behavior / gaps |
|---|---|
| I/O / output routing | XLR andLINE independently select MIX,PRE/POST,orCH. MIX includes USB playback; PRE/POST sends pre-effectsL/processedR; CH sends input2L/input1R. Ambience can feed both output groups,either,or neither. |
| Processing / input2 bypass | Input2 FX BYPASS retains **looper and ambience**. Mono send/return;direct mixing selectable when RETURN is connected. Not a fully dry bypass. |
| Factory / user memory | Ten editable effect/setup memories. Startup defaultsManual,configurable toMemory orMemoryStomp. Per-effect memory/manual selection allows some controls to remain at knob positions across recalls. Factory-filled count unknown. |
| Preset controls / saving | HoldEXIT+MEMORY,confirmMEMORY,choose destination,confirmagain. Recalled knobs use pickup behavior: value changes only after crossing the stored position. |
| USB audio / monitoring | USB direct-outOFF sends unit sound only tocomputer;host-processed return can feed outputs. ON restores local output. Dedicated driver required;not evidence of a full editor orUSB power. |
| Expression / live | External EXP targets output,ambience,orloop level. Three named footswitches: BOOST,DELAY,TUNER&MUTE;rotary/button inventory is documented below. |

[AD10-PDF]: https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/AD-10_eng04_W.pdf

### AD-10 — effect choices and loop operation

Checked2026-10-17 against [eng04_W][AD10-PDF],printed pp7,9–10.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / choices | Named choices above exclude bypass. Ambience selects reverberation length,not documented room/hall algorithms. Category counts do not establish a total simultaneous-block limit. |
| Looper / controls | DELAY+TUNER&MUTE enters loop operation;TUNER&MUTE records,plays,and overdubs. Stop requires two presses;clear requires holding LOOP at least2s while stopped. Sequence defaults record→play→overdub;record→overdub→play is selectable. Playback level50 is unity. Power-off phrase retention,undo/redo,and phrase export remain unverified. |

### AD-10 — feedback, input EQ and output-level options

Checked2026-10-18 against [eng04_W manual pp7–8,10–12][AD10-PDF]. No hardware operation or downloads.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / feedback filters | TwoNOTCH filters specify **A♯1–F7**; pressingbothNOTCHbuttons scansfeedback automatically. Individualnotchdepth1–20; REDUCTION independentlytargets low-frequencyfeedback. Rangeis acontrolspecification,not guaranteedeliminationofallfeedback or extraaudioinputs. |
| I/O / independent input EQ and phase | GUITAR IN1/2 canhaveindependentEQ; withinthatsetup FX LOOPbutton reverses selectedinputphase. EQsubparametersinclude **midQ0.5–16** andnoise-suppressionamount. This isnot documented freechainreordering or twocompleteindependenteffectsengines. |
| I/O / fixed output levels | XLRlevel andheadphonelevel eachselect **fixed0–99 orfollowOUTPUT LEVEL knob**. Thesearecontrolvalues,notdBu. They areseparatefromloopplaybacklevel andUSBinput/outputlevels. |
| Power / automatic shutdown | FactoryAutoOff shutsdownafter **10hours withoutplaying/operation**; disableoptionavailable. This doesnot resolve looppersistence afterpowerloss or replace thesix-houralkalineestimate. |

### AD-10 — system expression-volume screening (2026-10-28)

[Own eng04_W manual][AD10-PDF], printed pp11–12, places pedal-function assignment under System Settings: **EF→ov (OUTPUT LEVEL)**, alternatively AN (ambience) or LP (loop). This establishes a shared assignment, distinct from per-memory stomp-effect settings. Exact volume endpoints/location and held-position recall remain unverified. PHONES/XLR fixed-versus-follow-output settings and output routing remain separate coverage qualifications; no blanket bypass/tail/recorded-loop attenuation claim.

Own p5 confirms foot memory browsing both directions in Memory mode. L/PHONES is a **shared** output, not a separate headphone socket. Acoustic piezo/magnetic pickup blending is documented; ordinary electric-guitar tonal suitability remains unverified. Existing217W×161Dmm exceeds both preferred limits. Controller pinout/resistance remains unverified; no hardware test.

## EX-4 — partial no-treadle discovery lead

Checked2026-10-28;effects-only guitar/bass multi-effects,not a single-function pedal. **Outside active inventory pending exact Shopee hardware andcomplete required-field profile.** No ownership assertion.

| Field | Manufacturer evidence / qualification |

### EX-4 — original effect-family census and carryover limits

Checked2026-10-28 against original ©2026 Roland HTML Parameter Guide/Sound List andReference Manual;no explicit firmware minimum established.

**46selectable families**,derived by counting eachrow once in the [FX1–4 table](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/583875851637650315.html): COMPRESSOR, LIMITER, SLOW GEAR, BASS SLOW GEAR, PITCH SHIFT, HARMONIST, OCTAVE, BASS OCTAVE, HEAVY OCT, S-BEND, PEDAL BEND, TUNE DOWN, DEFRETTER, BASS DEFRETTER, AC. GTR SIM, AC RESO, AC BASS, SITAR SIM, GUITAR SYNTH, BASS SYNTH, OD/DS, PARA EQ, GEQ, ENHANCER, TOUCH WAH, BASS TOUCH WAH, AUTO WAH, WAH, ENV FILTER, FEEDBACKER, HUMANIZER, CHORUS, FLANGER, BASS FLANGER, PHASER, BASS PHASER, SCRIPT PH, CLASSIC-VIBE, ROTARY, VIBRATO, TREMOLO, PAN, RING MOD, SLICER, DELAY, REVERB. Samefamilies can occupy multipleFXslots. This is not the120-type marketingcensus:subtype counts stillneed reconciliation.

[Guitar Synth](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/666975243666986635.html) and[Bass Synth](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/593776779637701003.html) each offerSaw/Square andrequire single-note playing;guitarguide explicitly rejects chords andrequires muting previousstrings/clearattack. These are tracked-input synth effects,not generic “synth-like” presetnames orpolyphonic synthesis. [Preset11](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/637727371637742731.html) explicitly changes single-coil tone toacousticguitar. AC RESO instead processes anacoustic-electric pickup toward close-mic tone;AC BASS simulates acousticbass. This narrows theearlier synth/acoustic unknowns without implying captureimport.

[Carryover instructions](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_reference/en-US/581404299589796619.html) saydestinationmemory settings affect thedecay. Fornatural transitions,BOSS recommends matching effecttypes andchainarrangement across preceding/following memories,whilechangingparameters/on-off states. EnableCARRYOVER inMASTER and**save thememory**. This isconditional memory-transition behavior,not globalvolume assignment,universal gaplessswitching orverified EXPattenuation ofdecayingtails. Other unresolved profilefields andexactShopee hardware remainpending;EX-4 staysoutsideactivecounts. No downloads ordevice test.

### EX-4 — drive, delay, reverb and compressor subtype census

Checked 2026-10-28 against original ©2026 Roland parameter tables. These are choices within four families, not extra simultaneous blocks or a completed reconciliation of the advertised 120 types.

| Family | Complete TYPE enumeration in the checked page |
|---|---|
| [OD/DS](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/584045067637679499.html) | **35:** MID BOOST, CLEAN BST, TREBLE BST, NATURAL OD, WARM OD, BLUES OD, OVERDRIVE, CRUNCH, T-SCREAM, TURBO OD, CENTA OD, X-OD, DIST, A-DIST, FAT DS, LEAD DS, RAT, GUV DS, DIST+, X-DIST, METAL DS, METAL ZONE, HVY METAL, METAL CORE, OCT FUZZ, 60S FUZZ, MUFF FUZZ, BASS OD, X-BASS OD, BASS DS, BASS DI, SA DI DRIVE, HI BAND DRV, BASS MT, BASS FUZZ. |
| [DELAY](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/584607499637695627.html) | **13:** STANDARD, PAN, REVERSE, ANALOG, SPACE ECHO, BIN ECHO, GIGA DELAY, VINT.DIGITAL, SLOW ATTACK, SHIMMER, WARP, TWIST, GLITCH. |
| [REVERB](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/584640779637697163.html) | **8:** HALL S, HALL M, PLATE, ROOM S, ROOM L, AMBIENCE, SPRING, SHIMMER. |
| [COMPRESSOR](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/583960587637677963.html) | **6:** BOSS COMP, D-COMP, ORANGE, X-COMP, STEREO, X-BASS. |

Delay TIME is **1–2000 ms or BPM**, with type-dependent values and an explicit exception for TWIST. Tempo values outside the allowed span are synchronized at one-half or one-quarter instead. Footnote7 says TAP TIME applies only to **TAP**, but no TAP type appears in this page’s TYPE enumeration. Preserve this internal naming inconsistency; do not invent a fourteenth delay or silently substitute PAN. TRIGGER is stored Off when the memory is written, under the page’s type restrictions; it is not persistent phrase storage.

Reverb TIME is **0.1–10.0 s**, PRE-DELAY **0–200 ms**. Compressor sustain applies to all listed types except X-BASS, which instead exposes THRESHOLD; the common relative0–100 controls are not published milliseconds or dB. Bass-specific choices here corroborate shared guitar/bass suitability, not a new vocal classification. Remaining family subtypes, current-firmware completeness, volume coverage and exact Shopee hardware remain unresolved. Partial lead remains outside active counts; no downloads or hardware tests.

### EX-4 — pedal recall, jack modes and further subtype limits

This narrows earlier recall uncertainty. It does not set the pedal target, globally relocate Foot Volume, or establish power-on recall, endpoint precedence or bypass/tail attenuation. The same table makes **STEREO I/O versus SEND/RETURN mutually selected modes** for INPUT R/RETURN and OUTPUT R/SEND. The right jacks cannot simultaneously provide independent stereo I/O and a separate loop. BOSS instructs disconnecting and correctly reconnecting those cables when changing mode to avoid audio loops. COLOR=FULL gives a color display; the named single-color themes instead use black-and-white screen rendering with colored indicators. Those themes do not make the physical display monochrome-only.

### EX-4 — system output-level assignment and USB backup scope

Checked 2026-10-28. The original [TARGET list](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/632117771637723787.html) includes **MASTER → OUT LEVEL**, separate from FV position/min/max/curve. The [English MASTER page](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/631124107637712523.html) explicitly calls OUT LEVEL a system parameter shared by all memories. Combined with the documented [ASSIGN controls](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/632079883637723019.html), this supports a second configuration: **SW On; PREF SYSTEM; SOURCE EXP; ASSIGN MODE MANUAL; CATEGORY MASTER; TARGET OUT LEVEL**, with target-dependent MIN/MAX. This targets the system output parameter rather than the patch’s placed FV control. It is stronger evidence for a shared output-volume target, but not a hardware-tested mute or proof of action during ALL BYPASS. Competing assignments, routing coverage, startup recall and tails remain unverified.

**Language conflict:** English MASTER lists MEM LEVEL and OUT LEVEL **1–200**; [Japanese MASTER](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/ja-JP/631124107637712523.html) lists both **0–200**. Preserve both; minimum-zero availability and actual silence cannot be certified from the conflicting documents. Do not derive dB attenuation from these relative values. Earlier FV placement/range qualifications remain relevant to the FV-target configuration; they must not be silently applied as requirements of this distinct OUT LEVEL target.

The [original MIDI BULK DUMP instructions](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_reference/en-US/580586379589802763.html) establish **standard operating-system USB driver** use and a computer DAW/MIDI sequencer that can record SysEx. Transfer range separates SYSTEM, GEAR SUITE and U001–U128; retransmitting saved bulk data restores those settings. iPhone/iPad cabling is mentioned, but no dedicated mobile editor is established. This is documented MIDI backup/restore, not a USB audio interface, USB bus-power specification or BOSS Tone Studio editor. Exact OS-version matrix and power details remain unresolved. No backup, restore, reset, installation, download or hardware operation performed. EX-4 remains a partial lead outside active counts.

### EX-4 — exact onboard memory navigation and Gear Suite scope

Checked **2026-11-01** in the original ©2026 Roland HTML Reference Manual. No minimum firmware is stated on these pages.

| Field | Manufacturer instructions / qualification |

| Controls / onboard previous and next | [Memory-mode footswitch settings](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_reference/en-US/582644747589778699.html): select **MEMORY** mode, then its **MEM down/up** function. DOWN/1 selects the previous memory; UP/2 selects the next. Alternate **BYPASS/MEM up** assigns DOWN/1 to All Bypass and UP/2 to next memory, removing previous-memory stepping from that pair. FX1/FX2 and FX3/FX4 are other alternatives. These are selectable functions, not additional switches or evidence of current factory settings. |
| Memory / Gear Suite versus whole memories | [Gear Suite recall](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_reference/en-US/569265291589775883.html) distinguishes protected **P01–** effect settings from writable **U01–** settings. These belong to each effect type, not extra complete user/preset memories. Default list order is User then Preset; Favorites sorting prioritizes marked entries. The inspected prose does not publish the maximum slot count, so no10-slot total is adopted from review wording. |
| Memory / saving an effect setting | [Gear Suite saving](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_reference/en-US/581585419589799691.html) uses **GEAR SUITE WRITE**, selects an effect block and destination, permits a **12-character name**, and confirms with TYPE/VALUE. This saves an effect setting, not a global expression assignment or another simultaneous effect. |

Headphone provision and expression-volume range, bypass, tails and startup recall remain separate unresolved fields. The shared main LCD does not enter the individual-foot-LCD shortlist. ### EX-4 — pitch note handling and composite organ preset

Checked **2026-11-03** in original ©2026 Roland HTML; no firmware introduction minimum is stated.

- [HARMONIST](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/665436683666928139.html) explicitly requires **single notes**, muting other/previous strings and a clear attack. One/two generated voices and mono/stereo output do not change this restriction.

FAST/MEDIUM/SLOW trade response speed against modulation; MONO is another mode. The checked prose does not certify chord support for each mode or provide measured latency.
- [HEAVY OCT](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/583875851637650315.html) explicitly adds one/two octaves below **chords**. This does not establish polyphonic Guitar/Bass Synth tracking.
- [Preset53 MONO ORGAN](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/637727371637742731.html) combines COMP, OCTAVE, PITCH SHIFTER and ROTARY. It is a composite recipe, distinct from the separately documented single-note synth algorithms.

### EX-4 — memory-gap direct sound and noise detection

Checked **2026-11-04**, [manufacturer MASTER parameter page](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/631124107637712523.html), ©2026 HTML; firmware applicability unstated.

**SEAM LEVEL0–100** sets the direct sound produced instead of effect sound during the silence of memory switching. **CARRYOVER** separately controls whether effect sound remains across the change. SEAM is therefore not proof of uninterrupted processed sound, recorded-loop storage or retained delay/reverb tails. Neither entry establishes that the system OUT LEVEL assignment attenuates every transition path or ALL BYPASS.

The noise suppressor can detect **INPUT** or **NS INPUT**. The manufacturer advises NS INPUT in its illustrated spatial-effect arrangement to avoid eliminating delay-like sound; that diagram was not visually inspected here. High thresholds can suppress playing when the guitar volume is reduced. Detector choice is separate from the suppressor’s audio-chain placement and does not establish external-pedal volume coverage. The second suppressor’s controls are available only outside **SERIAL1** structure.

Earlier shared OUT LEVEL assignment, zero-valued range and EXP HOLD findings remain. Exact Shopee hardware, startup/output/bypass/tail coverage and installed behavior remain unverified. Partial lead stays outside active counts; no download, assignment, connection or device operation.

### EX-4 — switch-chord control and input-set scope wording

Checked **2026-11-05**, original ©2026 Roland HTML manuals; firmware applicability unstated.

- [Simultaneous-switch setting](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_reference/en-US/585615115589780235.html): the default **MEM/MAN** action toggles memory/manual mode when both onboard switches are pressed together. Setting that action **OFF** disables the mode toggle;this doesnot establish that both individual switch actions are suppressed,change the physical switch count ordisable remote mode selection.
- [INPUT SETTING reference](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_reference/en-US/581586571589800459.html) says the input set is chosen in the MASTER section of the memory. The [MASTER parameter table](https://static.roland.com/manuals/ex-4_parameter/en-US/631124107637712523.html) instead explicitly labels **INPUT SET1–10 a system parameter shared across all memories**. Preserve the wording difference;the explicit table supports shared selection,but per-memory recall is not independently established. Input conditioning is separate from EXP assignment andoutput-volume coverage.

EX-4 remains a partial lead outside active inventory. Exact Shopee hardware,complete software/power coverage andall-route pedal attenuation remain unresolved. No setting changed,download ordevice operation.

## GM-800

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. This processor qualifies through independent MFX architecture; it requires specialized input hardware for guitar/bass playing. No excluded specialist model is added.

Technical research snapshot: **2026-10-14**. All records remain partial; `?` means unverified, not No. This reclassification preserves earlier sources and uncertainties; it is not a fresh technical verification. Dimensions are W×D×H in millimetres; cables and pedal travel require additional clearance.

<!-- BOSS-GM-800 source begins -->

Included as a multi-instrument synthesizer with multiple independent MFX stages, not merely because it contains multiple synth sounds. Keep its specialized pickup requirements visible rather than treating it as a conventional analog-input guitar processor.

| Field | Preserved comparison evidence |
|---|---|
| Built-in / external expression | No built-in pedal; **2 CTL/EXP sockets**, up to 4 external switches |
| Rotary / footswitch / separate buttons | **6 / 4 / 10** |
| Dimensions / weight / size | **246×134×63 mm**, H65 with feet; **1.2 kg** excluding adapter; medium |
| Screen size / color / touchscreen | **? / ? / ?**; original display review still pending |
| Used IDR / new IDR | **? / ?**; no verified price observation retained for this model |
| Architecture | **4 Tone parts + 1 Rhythm part**; 1200+ tones, 70+ drum kits |
| Effect categories / choices | Each part has MFX with **93 types** and EQ; scene has **2 MFX**, each 93 types, plus reverb **7** and chorus/delay **9** types |
| Presets / memory | **150 editable Scenes, 100 factory-filled**; separate import-tone memory |
| Synth / acoustic | ZEN-Core instrument synth with instrument-style tones; exact acoustic-simulation distinction remains to be verified |
| IR / NAM / proprietary content | Roland Cloud expansions are proprietary tone content; user IR and NAM loading not established |
| Connections | **Serial GK IN/OUT**; 2×¼″ audio outputs, L shared with phones; 2×CTL/EXP; DIN MIDI IN/OUT; USB-B computer; USB-memory port; DC |
| Power / USB power | Supplied **PSB-1U adapter; 840 mA**; battery/USB bus power not listed |
| Configuration / USB functions | BOSS Tone Studio **Windows/macOS**; USB audio/MIDI, guitar-to-MIDI and re-synthesis |
| Main limitation | GK-5/GK-5B or compatible conversion required for guitar input; Serial GK is not an ordinary analog guitar jack |

The Serial GK connection uses the manufacturer's **BGK cable** and an appropriate divided pickup/interface. Older 13-pin GK equipment requires the appropriate **GKC-AD** conversion; a standard guitar cable is not a substitute. MIDI keyboards/controllers can also drive the sound engine. Primary source: [BOSS GM-800](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gm-800/).
<!-- BOSS-GM-800 source ends -->

### GM-800 — GK Audio is a separate serial stereo path

Checked **2026-11-04**: [manufacturer-authored Parameter Guide reproduction,edition03,©2023,printedpp9,18](https://www.manualzz.com/doc/81521733/boss-gm-800-guitar-synthesizer-owner-manual). Exact GM-800 title,parameter organization andGK Audio chapter;original body only,not host summary/FAQs. The [manufacturer manual index](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gm-800/owners_manuals/) lists the guide but its download page exposes a license gate;the attempted direct eng03 endpoint was inaccessible. No local download;this is not proof oflatest guide orinstalled firmware.

| Field | Verified scope / limitation |
|---|---|
| Routing / GK Audio | Stereo audio exchange between Serial GK devices. **OFF** disables it;**MIX** combines incoming serial audio with this unit’s stereo output forGK OUT;**OUT** sends this unit’s stereo output;**THRU** forwards incoming serial audio toGK OUT. These modes do not establish direct analog guitar input orpart-MFX processing ofincoming audio. |
| Memory / GK Audio overrides | Scene MASTER offers GK Audio Mode, GK Mix Level andGK Out Level with **SYSTEM** inheritance;levels otherwise0–200. System Mix/Out levels also0–200. These are serial-stream controls,not proof ofglobal analog-pedal volume across every preset/output. |

Normal-pickup OUTPUT MODE support from1.10 andGK Audio introduction1.20 remain distinct firmware-qualified features. Detailed normal-pickup/serial-audio MFX coverage remains unresolved;do not replace that unknown withNo orassign all93 MFX tothese paths. No device/MIDI operation.

### GM-800 — conditional expression recall and USB loopback

Checked **2026-11-05**, [manufacturer-authored Parameter Guide03 reproduction,©2023,printed pp17,20](https://www.manualzz.com/doc/81521733/boss-gm-800-guitar-synthesizer-owner-manual). Original document body only; current installed firmware untested. Direct manufacturer eng03 PDF remains inaccessible.

**Assign Hold:** Off recalls the scene’s saved EXP1/EXP2/GK VOL values. On retains values across scenes **only if their assigned parameters match**. GK VOL has an additional GK-Type qualification excluding GK-5/GK-5B. This is not physical-pedal polling, a global volume target, or evidence of universal output coverage.

**USB Audio Routing:** Standard mixes host playback with the instrument at hardware outputs, but sends only the GM-800 sound back to USB. Mix also sends host playback back to USB. Re-Synth uses recorded divided-pickup audio to retrigger the engine. Mix/Out levels each span0–127; these are USB levels,not EXP endpoints.

Tuner offers single,multi and True Temperament modes; TT supports regular tuning only. Reference pitch435–445Hz; Mute suppresses output, while Bypass passes the divided-pickup sound unchanged. Firmware1.20 added tuner modes; installed behavior,normal-pickup MFX coverage andglobal volume remain unresolved. No device operation ordownload.

### GP-10 — system controller assignment and recall are separate

Checked **2026-11-05**, [manufacturer owner manual e02,printedpp2–4,11,13](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GP-10_e02_W.pdf). Historical documentation,not installed-firmware verification.

- **System assignment:** SysCtl can replace default PATCH SETTING with a fixed function across patches. EXP2 is included;FOOT VOL andPATCH LEVEL are distinct available functions. This documents global function assignment,not just calibration. FOOT VOL position,endpoints,bypass,headphone,USB andtail coverage remain unverified. Built-in treadle excludes GP-10 fromthe no-treadle shortlist.
- **Recall:** Asgn Hld Sw separately enables/disables carrying expression/GK-volume state into the next patch. It doesnot establish startup position orfinal-output mute.
- **Navigation:** dedicated previous/next patch pedals are documented;system CTL PATCH UP/DOWN additionally supports configurable step distances. A ten-patch example isnot a fixed increment.
- **Output interaction:** plugging in headphones forces LINE/PHONES output mode,also affecting OUTPUT sound. This is output-system selection,not measured volume attenuation. GUITAR OUT carries normal-pickup audio.

External EXP orCTL3/4 uses one shared socket;simultaneous independent expression plus two switches isnot established. Voltage/polarity,complete knob/button census andcurrent qualified Indonesian offers remain open. No pedal,wiring,settings ordevice operation.

### GP-10 — named instrument models and explicit OSC poly mode

Checked **2026-11-05**, [manufacturer owner manual e02,printedpp6–8](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GP-10_e02_W.pdf). Historical named inventory;current installed content unverified.

| Modeling group | Exact manual names |
|---|---|
| Electric12 |CLA ST;MOD ST;TE;LP;P90;335;L4;RICK;LIPS;WIDE RANGE;BRIGHT HUM;FRETLESS |
| Acoustic9 |MA28;TRP-0;GB45;GB SML;GLD40;NYLON;RESO;BANJO;SITAR |
| Bass3 |JB;PB;FRETLESS |

These are alternative GK instrument models,not24 simultaneous tones or24 acoustic IRs. Bass modeling doesnot establish bass-input compatibility. Fretless guitar andbass are separate choices.

**OSC SYNTH explicitly offers Poly/Mono.** MONO sounds one note even with chord input;POLY supports per-string orlast-note-on-any-string portamento. This directly documents a poly mode;it isnot inferred fromtwo oscillators. Tracking quality,latency andexact note ceiling remain unmeasured.

OSC Hold modes separately accept-and-hold new notes,ignore new notes,oraccept new notes onalready-held strings. These are performance behaviors,not stored loops. WAVE SYNTH’s SAW/SQUARE waveform choices andlower-octave option do not independently establish its note ceiling. Existing GK andalternate-tuning restrictions remain. No device operation ordownload.

### GP-10 — pitch-mode restrictions and six USB routes

Checked **2026-11-05**, [manufacturer Parameter Guide e02,©2014,printedpp4,8](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GP-10_Parameter_Guide_e02_W.pdf). Document revision,not installed firmware.

**Pitch Shifter:** MONO requires single notes;FAST/MEDIUM/SLOW trade response speed against modulation,without explicit chord certification here. One/two generated voices andmono/stereo output are separate. **Harmonist:** up/down two-octave harmony withkey selection;note polyphony unverified. **Pedal Bend:** Position must be assigned toEXP orsimilar controller;note polyphony unverified. These share the selectable FX position,not three independently duplicated blocks.

| USB routing mode | Documented purpose / boundary |
|---|---|
| STANDARD |Backing playback monitored;only processor audio returns tocomputer. |
| MIX |Processor pluscomputer backing returns tocomputer. |
| DRY-GUITAR |Unprocessed guitar recording; six separate divided-string channels plus normal-pickup route. |
| RE-GUITAR/AMP I |Computer dry audio enters forremodeling/reamping. |
| RE-GUITAR/AMP II |Remodeling alongside computer backing playback. |
| DIRECT OFF |Processor audio goes tocomputer;DAW-processed audio feeds hardware OUTPUT. |

These routes do not establish USB-bus power,physical DIN MIDI,all-mode channel counts orglobal EXP attenuation. Driver/current-host compatibility andlatency remain untested. No download,recording,connection ordevice operation.

### GP-10 — current macOS matrix and regional adapter rating

Checked **2026-11-05** in [BOSS’s linked macOS compatibility matrix](https://www.boss.info/global/support/support_news/25090811m1/), **updated October30,2026**. The GP-10 has separate **Hardware & Driver** and **Exclusive Application** rows. Both mark **macOS15 Sequoia and26 Tahoe:Yes(Download)**;both mark **macOS27 Golden Gate:Under Testing**. These are the source’s explicit version/name labels andtest statuses,not aclaim that all macOS releases orprocessor architectures work.

**Two rotary controls:** one potentiometer andone encoder,each withone knob;the treadle’s internal position sensor isnot athird hand knob. **Seven separate buttons** are corroborated byseven keytops/seven tact switches andthe [owner manual’s editing controls,pp4–5](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GP-10_e02_W.pdf):MODELING/ALT TUNE,EFFECTS,SYSTEM,WRITE,EXIT andtwo page arrows. Arrow/function reuse doesnot add buttons.

**Four onboard pedal switches** remain as manufacturer-listed:patch previous/next andCTL1/2. The expression toe switch and rear power switch are separate fromthis K/F/B count. ### GP-10 — named FX hierarchy and dynamics choices

Checked **2026-11-05**, [manufacturer Parameter Guide e02,©2014,printed p3](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GP-10_Parameter_Guide_e02_W.pdf). Historical reference; installed equivalence untested. This expands the existing category totals without admitting the partial lead to active comparisons.

| Level | Named alternatives |
|---|---|
| FX16 | OD/DS; COMPRESSOR; LIMITER; EQ; T. WAH; PITCH SHIFTER; HARMONIST; PEDAL BEND; PHASER; FLANGER; TREMOLO; PAN; ROTARY; UNI-V; CHORUS; DELAY |
| OD/DS21 within FX | MID BOOST; CLEAN BOOST; TREBLE BOOST; CRUNCH; NATURAL OD; WARM OD; FAT DS; LEAD DS; METAL DS; OCT FUZZ; BLUES OD; OD-1; T-SCREAM; TURBO OD; DIST; RAT; GUV DS; DST+; METAL ZONE; ‘60S FUZZ; MUFF FUZZ |
| Compressor8 within FX | BOSS COMP; Hi-BAND; LIGHT; D-COMP; ORANGE; FAT; MILD; STEREO COMP |
| Limiter3 within FX | BOSS LIMITER; RACK 160D; VTG RACK U |

Subtypes occupy their parent FX choice: OD/DS,Compressor,Limiter,Pitch Shifter andHarmonist are not independent concurrent FX blocks. The separately documented Chorus,Delay,EQ andWah positions retain their own scope. Stereo Compressor describes audio channels,not input-note polyphony. T. WAH follows picking dynamics; it is filtering,not another oscillator synth.

The guide appears to swap Compressor Tone/Level descriptions: Tone is described as volume andLevel as tone. Preserve the inconsistency; physical control behavior remains untested. Full remaining-category names,concurrency,import formats andqualified Indonesian offers remain open. No download ordevice operation.

### GP-10 — separate effect inventories and Foot Volume routing

Checked **2026-11-05**, [manufacturer owner manual e02,©2014,printed pp10–11](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GP-10_e02_W.pdf). Historical reference, not an installed-firmware or simultaneous-load test.

| Separate position | Named choices |
|---|---|
| Wah 6 | CRY WAH; VO WAH; FAT WAH; LIGHT WAH; 7-STRING WAH; RESO WAH |
| Chorus 3 | MONO; STEREO1; STEREO2 |
| Delay 10 | SINGLE; PAN; STEREO; DUAL-S; DUAL-P; DUAL-L/R; REVERSE; ANALOG; TAPE; MODULATE |
| Reverb 7 | AMBIENCE; ROOM; HALL1; HALL2; PLATE; SPRING; MODULATE |

Delay **DUAL-S** is series, **DUAL-P** parallel, **DUAL-L/R** independent left/right. DUAL-S/P each allow1–1000ms per delay; Analog1–2000ms andTape1–3400ms are different limits. STEREO sends dry left and wet right; its name does not mean independent stereo-input processing. RESO WAH models resonant filtering, not an additional tracking synth.

FX Chain permits reordering. **Foot Volume cannot be turned off**; it has heel/toe endpoints and selectable curve, separate from Patch Level. The guide does not establish universal headphone/USB/tail coverage. Normal-pickup Cable Sim compensates the short GK pickup cable; the manual specifies Off for ordinary GUITAR IN. This is cable-response compensation, not instrument synthesis. Other format,market and firmware gaps remain; partial lead stays outside active counts. No download ordevice operation.

### GS-10 — Assign Hold recall and tuner bypass boundary

Checked 2026-10-28 against the retained [manufacturer-authored manual](https://manualzz.com/doc/2026468/boss-gs-10-guitar-effects-system-owner%E2%80%99s-manual), printedpp64–66. Exact original GS-10 body; no hardware test.

| Field | Documented behavior / limitation |
|---|---|
| Expression / Assign Hold On | Carries ordinary Assign operational state across patches. Volume→volume example follows current pedal angle; volume→wah uses destination patch volume while wah follows the angle. This is target-dependent recall,not a global assignment lock. |
| Expression / Assign Hold Off | Destination volume starts at its saved patch value; subsequent pedal movement changes it. Assign Hold does not operate with Toggle source mode. Fixed SYSTEM Foot Volume behavior is not independently resolved by these Assign examples. |
| Bypass / tuner | Tuner offers **Mute** or **Bypass**;Bypass sends input without processing and is the documented factory default. No explicit expression-volume exception is stated here,so do not borrow GT-Pro’s tuner-volume guarantee. Headphone,tail and recorded-loop coverage remain unknown. |

Physical knob Current Setting mode is a separate pickup behavior,not expression recall. The documented global pedal role remains supported;range precedence,universal output attenuation and actual M-VAVE compatibility remain unverified. Partial lead stays outside active counts. No local file downloaded ordevice operated.

### GS-10 — creative tracking and instrument-simulation boundaries

Checked **2026-11-04**, [manufacturer-authored original manual reproduction](https://manualzz.com/doc/2026468/boss-gs-10-guitar-effects-system-owner%E2%80%99s-manual), **03342912,03-8-1N,©2003 BOSS**,printedpp37,43–44,50–52. Exact title/copyright/chapter identity verified;host-generated overview/FAQ excluded. Official static PDF endpoint inaccessible;no installed-firmware ortracking-quality test.

**Guitar Synth requires single notes**,with other strings muted andclear attacks. **Square/Saw** generate internal waveforms fromdetected pitch/attack;**Brass/Bow** shape inputaudio. Hold applies only toSquare/Saw;four waveform choices are notfour-note tracking.

**Tone Modify S→AC/H→AC** processes single-coil/humbucker electric tones into acoustic-guitar tones;**P→AC** separately treats piezo input. Hollow-body modes andBass Simulator aredistinct;the latter advises avoiding chords. These are effects,not acoustic IR loading.

**Pitch Shifter Fast/Medium/Slow accepts chords;Mono doesnot. Harmonist requires single notes** despite one/two-voice mono/stereo outputs. Synth,Harmonist,PitchShifter andBassSimulator compete forFX2;ToneModify occupiesFX1. Availability isnot simultaneous access toallthesechoices. Older sourceunknowns are superseded only forthese exact capabilities.

Shopee searches yieldedno qualified exact GS-10 hardware;no absence orprice claim. Global FV endpoints/recall/outputcoverage,initialUserfill,modernsoftware andremaining subtypecensus stayopen. No download,device operation orownership assertion.

### GT-1 — original USD and technical provenance

Research date **2026-10-14**. Technical sources are the linked manufacturer's specifications, including current firmware-dependent figures. **Partial records**: `?` means unverified. Dimensions are **W × D × H in mm**; `/max` gives the maximum height with the pedal raised. `K/F/B` = rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons, excluding the expression toe switch and rear power/mode switches. Price columns are **USD US-market listed/asking observations**, not statistically estimated fair values; see sources below.

Model totals have different inclusion rules. ME-90's selectable alternatives are not all simultaneously present on its panel. A missing NAM claim is not converted into a definitive “No” without model/firmware documentation.

Price retrieval date: **2026-10-14**. Indexed older listings are explicitly less reliable than current, purchasable offers. No guarantee of stock, local Indonesian pricing, delivery cost or completed-sale value.

### GT-1 — selector-count qualifications

Checked2026-10-17 against [own parameter guide][GT1-G], pp11–15. Delay choices: Standard,Pan,Reverse,Analog,Tape,Modulate,TeraEcho. Reverb slot: Ambience,Room,Hall1,Hall2,Plate,Spring,Modulate,**Delay**. Pedal: CryWah,VoWah,FatWah,LightWah,7StrWah,ResoWah,PedalBend. PedalBend is single-note,±24semitones; chords unsupported. Main delay reaches2000ms,FX SubDelay1000ms,reverb-slot Delay650ms. A following mono effect/COSM amp collapses stereo delay. Counts represent alternatives, not extra concurrent blocks; nested compressor/limiter/etc. types still need separate accounting.

### GT-1 — nested selector evidence

Checked2026-10-17, [own guide][GT1-G] pp6–10: Compressor8 (BOSS,Hi-Band,Light,D-Comp,Orange,Fat,Mild,Stereo); Limiter3 (BOSS,Rack160D,VtgRackU); ToneModify8 includes Resonator1/2/3 separately; GuitarSimulator8 pickup/body transformations; AcousticProcessor4 (Small,Medium,Bright,Power); Phaser4 (4/8/12Stage,BiPhase). T.Wah has LPF/BPF modes and Up/Down response; Chorus has Mono/Stereo1/Stereo2; SubDelay has Mono/Pan. Mode switches, continuous parameters and nested types must not be indiscriminately summed with top-level effects. Harmonist requires single-note playing; this restriction is not generalized to every pitch effect.

GT-1 owner-manual follow-up2026-10-17: own ©2016 manual pp2/8 read through web text. Battery fallback and backup scope are explicit; no USB bus-power statement was located. Absence of that statement is not proof USB power is impossible. No software, manual file or firmware downloaded; no device configuration changed.

### GT-001 — input selection and practice functions, 2026-10-17

[Official parameter guide](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf), 2014, printed pp. 33/39: targeted search-index text recovered despite full-reader size limit. No visual inspection claimed.

| Field | Verified behavior / limit |
|---|---|
| I/O / input routing | INPUT SELECT chooses **GUITAR or MIC; both cannot operate simultaneously**. Each selected input passes through the effects. Dual effect chains do not establish independent simultaneous guitar/vocal processing. Adjusting MIC LEVEL or PHANTOM can interrupt sound. |
| Looper / drums / practice | **Metronome 40–250 BPM**, selectable meters and level; output through OUTPUT/PHONES. Its tempo changes MASTER BPM. This is a metronome, not evidence of a drum-pattern library. Looper remains unverified. |
| Tuner / live | Reference 435–445 Hz; MUTE silences output, BYPASS removes effects, THRU retains the current effect sound while tuning. These mode names do not establish analog true-bypass circuitry. |

### GT-001 — USB paths and specified supply, 2026-10-17

| Field | Verified behavior / limit |
|---|---|
| USB audio / routing | [Official parameter guide, p. 34](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf), indexed original text: PRIMARY sends effects; its computer return mixes at the final stage. SECONDARY sends dry input; its return enters the effect-chain beginning for reamping. Both outputs are available together. Four recording channels are corroborated below; exact playback count remains unverified. |
| USB / level controls | [Own p34](#gt-001--usb-level-controls-and-aux-monitoring-boundary): PRIMARY MIX.LVL/EFX.OUT and SECONDARY DRY.OUT each0–200%;SECONDARY TO.EFX−20…+20dB. These have separate routing roles,not a common expression-master range. |
| USB / monitoring | PRIMARY LOOP BACK sends computer playback back with processed sound. DIRECT MONITOR resets ON at startup and cannot be saved. Loopback is an audio-routing function, not a phrase looper. |
| Power / adapter identity | [GT-001-specific BOSS support notice](https://www.boss.info/jp/support/by_product/gt-001/support_documents/36a0d74b-856b-417a-862c-ae72c8f9e0bf/) specifies **PSA-100**. Its [current manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/jp/products/psa-100/) gives **9 V DC, 500 mA maximum output**, 100–240 V AC, 50–60 Hz input. Adapter capacity differs from GT-001’s 240 mA draw. Polarity remains unverified; do not assume an old adapter shares the current mains range. |

### GT-001 — own-product category counts, 2026-10-17

| Field | Verified count / restriction |
|---|---|
| Processing / preamp and drive | Counting the [model-specific published lists][GT001-P] gives **28 PREAMP choices** and **23 OD/DS choices**, including each list’s Custom entry. These are alternatives, not 28 simultaneous amps or 23 drive blocks. |
| Processing / FX1-FX2 choices | **35 distinct top-level choices across both slots**; Overtone and Tera Echo are FX2-only, so **FX1 has 33 / FX2 has 35**. This is a union count, not 35 choices in each slot or 35 simultaneous effects. Nested choices within algorithms remain uncounted. |
| Processing / nested drive restriction | A-DIST is available in the main OD/DS block and the Sub OD/DS algorithm of FX2; do not assign it to FX1’s Sub OD/DS. Aggregate simultaneous ceiling and remaining category counts still unverified. |

### GT-001 — MIDI-document category evidence, 2026-10-17

[Official MIDI implementation](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-100_GT-001_MIDI_Imple_e01_W.pdf), April 30, 2014: cover explicitly identifies GT-001 v1.00 and GT-100 v2.00. Shared entries apply only where model exclusions do not intervene. Text inspection, no execution/download.

|---|---|
| Processing / reverb | P27 enumerates **seven**: Ambience, Room, Hall1, Hall2, Plate, Spring, Modurate (source spelling). Time 0.1–10 s; pre-delay 0–500 ms. Choices, not seven blocks. |
| Processing / chorus | P27 gives **three modes**: Mono, Stereo1, Stereo2; not three independent chorus slots. |
| Processing / delay conflict | P27 names **ten delay types**, but encodes TYPE as 00–0A (11 values); the eleventh is unnamed. Preserve discrepancy. Main time 1–2000 ms; dual sections 1–1000 ms; Sub Delay separately 1–1000 ms. |
| Processing / nested compressor | P19/23 enumerate **eight Advanced Comp subtypes** inside FX1/FX2; not eight extra processing slots or evidence that main COMP has eight types. |
| Looper / I/O exclusions | Shared document explicitly marks phrase-loop, send/return and amp-control entries **GT-100 only**. Those controls do not establish corresponding GT-001 capabilities. |

### GT-001 — external patch navigation and headphone-load scope

Checked **2026-11-04** in [indexed manufacturer Parameter Guide, printedp25](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf), its exact GT-001 CTL/EXP chapter; corroborating [manufacturer product specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-001/) and [original English owner manual pp2–4 reproduced on Manualzz](https://manualzz.com/doc/4573509/boss-gt-001-guitar-effects-processor-owner-manual). The latter has the GT-001 title, ©2014 BOSS and matching English section/page structure; multilingual/other-model boilerplate and host-generated answers are not used as specifications. No visual census.

**NUM INC / NUM DEC** moves to the adjacent higher/lower patch. The guide requires CTL1/2 preference **SYSTEM**, or the same function saved in every participating patch. It triggers on pedal press regardless of MOMENT/TOGGLE. **FAV INC/DEC** cycles A–D favorites, a different navigation scope. These are external controls, not proof of onboard preset footswitches. [Exact-model BOSS support article](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/360061945112), updated2022-01-18, explicitly says external footswitches and expression cannot be connected simultaneously. Its FS-7 example maps A→CTL2/NUM INC and B→CTL1/NUM DEC, both with SYSTEM preference and OFF/ON endpoints. A single FS-5U controls CTL1 only. This establishes the shared-port restriction, not generic third-party wiring compatibility. This supersedes the earlier external sequential-navigation unknown only.

Owner manual p4 calls A–D **buttons** for immediate favorite recall; holding one assigns the chosen patch. A photo/search description of footswitch-style buttons is not accepted as a physical footswitch count. Full panel/treadle census remains unresolved.

Product PHONES **44Ω output impedance**, **recommended load≥44Ω**, and **−10dBu nominal level** are distinct specifications. They do not establish milliwatts, maximum level or volume-pedal coverage. Main line outputs have their own2kΩ/≥10kΩ ratings. Older sources and the shop-card ask remain qualified; no current stock, firmware or measured performance claim. No download, connection or device operation.


### GT-001 — USB level controls and AUX monitoring boundary

Checked **2026-11-04**. [Manufacturer2014 Parameter Guide,printedp34,indexed original text](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf): **PRIMARY MIX.LVL0–200%** controls computer-return audio mixed at the final stage; **EFX.OUT0–200%** controls processed recording level. **SECONDARY TO.EFX−20…+20dB** controls computer audio entering the effect chain; **DRY.OUT0–200%** controls dry recording output. These are distinct taps/gains,not four published expression-pedal ranges. Parameter coverage doesnot resolve the directional playback-channel census orfull-output EXP attenuation. Full original PDF,including alternate GT-001_PG endpoint,exceeds reader size limit;no download.

The manufacturer-authored [English owner manual,p2](https://manualzz.com/doc/4573509/boss-gt-001-guitar-effects-processor-owner-manual),©2014 withmatching GT-001 chapters, directs **AUX playback volume to the player’s own controller**. That instruction doesnot prove a particular internal AUX tap orcoverage by OUTPUT LEVEL,FV,headphones orUSB recording. Host-generated FAQs andother-model compliance boilerplate excluded.

Exact Shopee searching surfaced the retained Standard Music shop-card only;no refreshed condition/stock ornewitem claim. Existing global-FV assignment evidence remains;range-storage,placement,recall andtail coverage remain qualified. No device,driver orsignal test.

### GT-001 — PATCH LEVEL differs from FOOT VOLUME

Checked **2026-11-05**, [manufacturer Parameter Guide, printedp26](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf), corroborated by its [2014 manufacturer-authored GT-001 parameter text](https://manualzz.com/doc/748967/boss-gt-001-guitar-effects-processor-owner-manual). EXP FUNC also lists **PAT LV (PATCH LEVEL)**, with separate **MIN/MAX0–200**, visible when that target is selected. These are not FV’s0–100 endpoints. The availability footnote literally requires **PERFORMANCE=SYSTEM (p35)**; that chapter labels the settings **PREFERENCE**. This terminology discrepancy remains unresolved. Do not treat PATCH LEVEL as an independently verified replacement setup or transfer its range to FV. Neither target establishes AUX/USB/headphone, bypass or tail coverage. No hardware test or installed-firmware claim.

### GT-1B — creative index and algorithm-specific note handling

Checked **2026-11-05**, [manufacturer Parameter Guide/Sound List, English02, printed pp6,8–9,11–12](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-1B_parameter_eng02_W.pdf),29 PDF pages. This supplements the existing record; it is not a complete fresh profile or an installed-firmware test. The previously recorded1.02 tracking improvements remain separately firmware-qualified.

| Field | Exact-model creative evidence |
|---|---|
| Synth / note handling | BASS SYNTH offers SAW and SQUARE. It requires single notes, muting previous/unused strings and clear attacks; sensitivity varies with pickup and bass-tone settings. These are two waveforms within one algorithm, not two-note polyphony. |
| Pitch / separate algorithms | PITCH SHIFTER spans **−24…+24 semitones**. HARMONIST and PEDAL BEND explicitly reject chords. OCTAVE adds one/two octaves below; chord handling for Octave and Pitch Shifter remains unverified. |
| Synth / processing distinctions | OVERTONE adds harmonics; HUMANIZER switches vowels by picking or automatically. RING MOD has Normal/Intelli modes; **Intelli requires single notes**. These are not additional Bass Synth engines. SLOW GEAR shapes attacks. |
| Synth / acoustic-bass distinction | DEFRETTER simulates fretless bass. BASS SIMULATOR has seven pickup/body transformations, including single-coil/humbucker to acoustic bass. Acoustic-bass processing is not acoustic-guitar simulation. |
| Processing / selection limits | These FX families are alternatives in FX1/FX2; PEDAL FX is separate. Listing available effects does not promise simultaneous access to all of them or measured tracking performance. |
| IDR / additional CH MUSIC hardware ask | **Rp4.550.000**, [CH MUSIC,shop195817632/item25052166296](https://shopee.co.id/BOSS-GT-1B-Bass-Effects-Processor-Efek-Pedal-Bass-BOSS-GT1B-i.195817632.25052166296), KotaJakartaPusat. Last-week indexed exact GT-1B hardware title; direct page cache-missed. Condition, selected variant, working state, firmware, bundle/adapter, warranty, stock, shipping/tax and checkout total remain unknown. Cached same-day delivery is not stock confirmation; up-to-Rp10.000 late compensation is not deducted. Separate from the older Hurtrock/Cityland/Rockpizz observations. |

Bass-only membership and ownership status are unchanged. No preset, firmware or software downloaded; no audio/controller test. The broader creative index reuses existing catalogue evidence and does not certify every recorded processor.

Ring Modulator: Normal/Intelligent. | [Manual p46][GT3-M] |

Manual recommends assigning search to CTL; manual frequency/depth settings are also available. | [Manual p39][GT3-M] |
| Categories / reverb | **Five alternatives**: Room 1/2, Hall 1/2, Plate. Reverb time **0.1–10.0 seconds**; pre-delay **0–100 ms**; low cut 55–800 Hz; high cut 700 Hz–11 kHz or Flat; density 0–10. These are choices/settings within the reverb section. | [Manual p56][GT3-M] |
| Categories / dedicated chorus | **Mono or Stereo**: Mono sends the same chorus to L/R; Stereo sends different chorus signals. Pre-delay **0–40 ms**; Rate/Depth/Level 0–100; BPM-synchronized rate available. Depth 0 supports doubling. High Cut table lists 700 Hz–11 kHz/Flat, but its explanatory paragraph describes low-cut behavior; source inconsistency retained. This section is separate from MOD 2×2 Chorus. | [Manual p55][GT3-M] |

Speaker simulation activates with global Line (Headphones); AC Guitar flat-amp simulation instead switches off in that setting. | [Manual p44][GT3-M] |
| Processing / preamp control exceptions | Match Drive ignores Middle. Match/Voxy Presence works as a high-frequency cut, displayed 0…−100. Depending on type, setting Bass/Middle/Treble all to zero may silence output. These are model-specific control behaviors. | [Manual p44][GT3-M] |
| Processing / programmable effect order | Manual explicitly permits changing effect connection order through Master → Effect Chain; insert the chosen processor at the cursor. Noise Suppressor is selected through Master and Foot Volume through Pedal/Assign. Save the resulting order using Write. This proves programmable order, not duplicated instances or a numerical simultaneous ceiling. | [Manual section 2, connection-order procedure][GT3-M] |
| Categories / delay | **Single or Tap**; Tap divides delay between stereo outputs. Delay time **0–1800 ms**, fine adjustment **0–20 ms**, with per-patch Master BPM synchronization. Tap Time **0–100%** sets right-channel time relative to left at 100%. Feedback 0–100; high cut 700 Hz–11 kHz or Flat; effect level 0–120. | [Manual pp54–55][GT3-M] |
| Processing / intelligent ring restriction | Intelligent Ring Modulator follows detected input pitch and requires single-note playing. Normal mode instead uses the internal oscillator. | [Manual p54][GT3-M] |
| Live / control assignments | **Eight assignments per patch** map controllers to parameters with target minimum/maximum, source mode and active range. **Four user assignment settings** can be reused across patches; these are not four extra sound patches. Save changes with Write. | [Manual p26][GT3-M] |
| Expression / virtual pedal triggers | Internal and Wave Pedal are virtual controllers. Internal Pedal can trigger on patch selection, built-in expression low/mid/high positions, CTL, external expression or SUB CTL1/2. Position thresholds do not establish a physical toe switch. Momentary footswitches support hold-dependent control; latch switches cannot provide that behavior. | [Manual section 2, Internal Pedal / active range][GT3-M] |
| Memory / reusable effect settings | Quick Setting stores **up to5 user settings for each effect-select button** for reuse acrosspatches. | I/O | Input, L/Mono and R outputs, stereo mini headphones, send/return, shared SUB EXP/SUB CTL1,2, MIDI In/Out; adapter input. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Conversion | **24-bit AF A/D,64×oversampling;20-bit D/A,128×oversampling;44.1kHz**. AF combines differently scaled converter streams;not proof of24-bit D/A orUSB recording. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |

## GT-6B — partial bass discovery lead

Checked 2026-11-04; discontinued exact-model bass multi-effects. **Partial lead outside the 402 active records; ownership not established.** Not GT-6 guitar. Sources: [US manufacturer](https://www.boss.info/us/products/gt-6b/), [Japan manufacturer](https://www.boss.info/jp/products/gt-6b/), [original owner manual, ©2002](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-6B_OM.pdf). Manual evidence does not identify installed/latest firmware.

| Field | Verified finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| Input/expression | Ordinary bass input; built-in expression and toe switch; shared SUB EXP/SUB CTL1,2. External wiring and global-volume assignment remain unverified. |
| Controls/headphones | Japan text names Number1–4, Patch Up/Down and Bypass/CTL; toe switch separate. Fifteen advertised panel knobs are not a complete rotary census. Visual switch/button counts pending. PHONES present; onboard patch up/down documented separately. |
| Display/memory | Shared backlit16×2-character LCD plus two-character7-segment LED; individual footswitch LCDs not established. Diagonal/color/touch unknown. 120 patches:80 writable user+40 protected preset; initial user fill remains unverified (manual pp14,23). |
| Size/power | 515W×261D×75H mm;4.7kg excluding adapter. Exceeds200×140mm preference. AC14V BRC adapter,800mA; not9VDC. DC polarity inapplicable to specified AC supply; plug size/battery/USB operation unverified. |
| Ports | INPUT; OUTPUT L/MONO,R; PHONES; XLR L/MONO,R/DIRECT; coax digital out; SUB EXP/SUB CTL1,2; MIDI IN/OUT; adapter. No USB listed; no USB-audio inference. |
| Source conflict | US XLR impedance600kΩ/300kΩ versus Japan600Ω; retained unresolved rather than silently corrected. |
| Market/software | No qualified exact Shopee Indonesia hardware offer from this search; new/used IDR unknown, not evidence of market absence. Editor platforms, IR/NAM/capture and remaining category census unverified. |

Creative processing: manual pp33–43 separates FX1 and FX2 alternatives. Bass Synth Square/Saw tracks single notes; Brass/Bow shapes input, with single-note attack detection when Attack Trig is on. Chord support otherwise remains unverified. Synth Hold applies to Square/Saw. Pitch Shifter spans±24 semitones: Fast/Medium/Slow accept chords; Mono and Harmonist require single notes. Slow modes trade response for reduced modulation. FX1 includes Octave, Enhancer, Slow Gear, Defretter and Ring Mod; FX2 alternatives include Harmonist, Pitch Shifter, Pedal Bend, Auto Slap, Humanizer and Bass Synth. These are not all simultaneous. Defretter is distinct from acoustic simulation, which remains unverified. Manual p27 places Sound On Sound as an alternative in REV/DLY/SOS, not a free additional looper block. Tracking quality/latency were not measured. Synth/NS placement prose on p43 appears internally inconsistent; no corrected routing recipe is inferred.

### GT-6B — memory and external-control scope

Checked 2026-11-04 against the [©2002 owner manual](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-6B_OM.pdf), pp11–14,23,50–51,61–62. Preset edits save only to User destinations, overwriting their previous contents. Power-on recalls the last selected patch.

External-pedal instructions specify EV-5 or FV-300L+PCS-33, pedal minimum at MIN. The documented volume procedure sets Sub CTL1 Func to Assignable, enables one of eight Assigns and selects SUB EXP FV. This demonstrates patch assignment, **not all-preset global volume**. Expression Pedal Hold carries operational status into the next patch's assigned function; a wah assignment remains wah. Toggle-source assignments do not use Hold. Exact wiring pinout, global volume availability, range, bypass, headphones and tail/looper attenuation remain unverified.

Rear OUTPUT LEVEL controls quarter-inch outputs and headphones; this does not prove XLR attenuation. Manual p61 says delay/reverb are interrupted during patch changes; universal spillover is not established. This remains a partial discovery lead outside active counts.

Also fixes FX CHAIN meter cursor tracking; improves chorus-mode transitions and category-keyword refresh. These are version-specific fixes, not proof of universal gapless switching. | [Japanese release notes][G10-U] |
| Firmware / memory maintenance | Update preserves user memory; BOSS recommends Librarian or bulk-dump backup. Transfer uses MIDI or USB with the dedicated driver and a SysEx-capable SMF utility; UpdSMF is an update transport, not a patch editor. | [Japanese release notes][G10-U] |
| Editor / Windows restrictions | Librarian1.00 manages user-patch collections; official page lists Windows XP/Vista/7/8/8.1/10. Its reference to an Editor does not identify or verify a separate full editor. Driver1.0.0 supports Windows10 32/64-bit, excludes Mobile, virtual Windows and Windows on Mac; USB3 ports must support USB2. Roland support updated2025-05-19 explicitly says **Windows11 unsupported**. This concerns the GT-10 driver, not proof every standard-audio mode fails. | [Librarian][G10-L]; [Driver][G10-D]; [Windows support][G10-T] |
| USB audio / recording modes | Standard mode uses the OS driver but **cannot use USB MIDI**; MIDI requires Advanced mode and the dedicated driver. Changing driver mode requires restart. Official support specifies a USB A–B cable, establishing USB-B on the unit. Direct Monitor controls GT-10 sound at OUTPUT/PHONES, resets On at power-up and can be controlled by ASIO2 software with the special driver. Off requires computer monitoring to hear the guitar. | [Indexed manual pp91,93][G10-M]; [Windows support][G10-T] |
| External control / assignments | Eight ASSIGN1–8 mappings. EXP1,EXP2,EXPSW,CTL1–4 can independently follow patch or system settings; system overrides ignore rather than erase patch settings. Switch behavior supports toggle/momentary. MIDI CC, input level, internal timed pedal and tempo-synced wave pedal are control sources. Foot volume is movable in-chain, distinct from post-effects overall volume. | [Workshop pp3–5,9–12][G10-W] |
| Looper / drums / live | Manufacturer Q&A confirms **38s mono /19s stereo**, overdubbing and **no saving the recorded performance**. This supports the workshop38s figure; Finnish product40s remains a conflicting regional claim. BANK pedals operate basic looping; additional assignments extend control. Manual p60 allows retaining bank switching by assigning loop control to CTL1–4; Clear Only erases immediately, while Mute/Clear first mutes and a second press erases. Drums/scenes/trails unverified. | [Q&A][G10-Q]; [Workshop pp2,5][G10-W]; [Regional product][G10-F]; [Indexed manual p60][G10-M] |

### GT-10B loop routing and bass algorithms

Checked 2026-10-18 against the manufacturer-authored 2008 manual, revision G6027127R0, as mirrored document text. No generated mirror FAQ, file download or device test used.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / external loop modes | Normal inserts the external device in series. Direct Mix blends return with the signal entering SEND/RETURN. Branch Out sends that signal out and disregards RETURN; its placement determines what reaches SEND. Send/return levels each use 0–200 display units, not documented dB. | [Own manual p131][G10B-T] |
| Synth / bass-simulator choices | BASS SIM has seven types: passive→active, active→passive, single-coil→humbucker, humbucker→single-coil, solid→hollow, single-coil→acoustic bass and humbucker→acoustic bass. Body 0–100 applies to the hollow/acoustic conversions; lower values approach piezo-like tone. These are choices within one algorithm. | [Own manual p109][G10B-T] |
| Synth / waveforms | BASS SYNTH offers Saw and Square waveforms, separate synth/direct levels and envelope-filter controls. Both waveforms belong to the same single-note algorithm; they do not establish two simultaneous synth engines. | [Own manual p110][G10B-T] |


| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Power / approved supply polarity | Named PSB-1U is specified by Roland as **9V, center-negative/sleeve-positive, 2000mA capacity**. This establishes compatible polarity; GT-10B device draw remains800mA. Regional mains/plug label must be checked separately. | [Own product][G10B-P]; [Roland supply specifications][G10B-PS] |
| I/O / connector sizes | INPUT, main OUTPUT, SEND/RETURN and AMP CONTROL are quarter-inch; PHONES stereo quarter-inch; EXP2/CTL3,4 quarter-inch TRS; SUB outputs XLR. USB connector type remains unresolved. | [Own product][G10B-P] |
| I/O / electrical levels | Nominal INPUT/RETURN−10dBu; input impedances1MΩ/220kΩ. Main OUTPUT−10/+4dBu,2kΩ; SEND−10dBu,3kΩ; SUB output600Ω. Nominal levels do not establish maximum headroom. | [Own product][G10B-P] |


| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Editor / Windows driver scope | **Driver1.0.0: Windows10 32/64-bit**, excluding Mobile. Page describes automatic internet driver delivery on connection; current delivery was not tested. Virtual Windows and Windows on Mac are unsupported. USB3 requires USB2 compatibility; try USB2 if malfunctioning. This driver page does not establish full-editor or Windows11 support. | [Official Windows driver page][G10B-W10] |
| Editor / macOS driver scope | **Driver1.0.3: macOS10.13/10.14** on Mac with onboard USB. Existing10.13 installation can be used on10.14; installer may still display10.13 wording. This does not establish newer macOS/Apple Silicon compatibility or librarian/editor support. No package downloaded or installed. | [Official Mac driver page][G10B-MAC] |

### GT-10B — channel modes and crossover

Checked2026-10-18 in the manufacturer-authored2008 manual G6027127R0, printedp133, via [manual body][G10B-T]; no generated FAQ or download.

| Field | Verified behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / channel modes7 | **Single, Dual Mix, Dual L/R, Freq Divide, Dynamic SW, Dynamic A, Dynamic B**. Single uses selected A/B; Dual Mix combines both; Dual L/R sends A left andB right. These are routing choices,not seven effects or a simultaneous-block count. |
| Processing / frequency split | Freq Divide assigns lows toA andhighs toB; crossover **100Hz–4.00kHz**. This enables frequency-separated processing,not an additional synth algorithm. |
| Processing / dynamic routing | Dynamic SW selectsB with stronger picking. Dynamic A/B changes the named channel’s level with input dynamics while the other remains fixed. Sensitivity/release are0–100 display units;these do not specify attack/release milliseconds. |
