### GT-10B — split outputs and conditional patch trails, 2026-10-18

[Manufacturer-authored2008 manual G6027127R0](https://manualzz.com/doc/51314063/boss-audio-systems-music-pedal-gt-10b-owner-s-manual), printedpp68–72; manual body only,not generated mirror FAQ.

| Field | Documented behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / XLR wet-direct split | SUB OUTPUT mode L/R sends stereo normally. **Effect/Direct sends the processed stereo mix as mono to XLR L,and pre-effect mono to XLR R**. INPUT SELECT,INPUT LEVEL andPRES. still affect the direct path;it is not necessarily untouched pickup signal. DIGITAL OUT andUSB send the same digital audio source (p68),not proof of the USB bit depth. |
| Looper / live patch-change trails | **Fast discards the previous delay/reverb decay; Smooth can retain it**. Conditions include matching effect-chain placement,identical delay type/time,and compatible other-effect states/parameters. The manual warns tails may still fail in some cases. Smooth switching may be delayed **one tempo beat**;this is not unconditional gapless switching or a fixed millisecond latency. |

## GT-1000

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. Full-size GT-1000 is distinct from GT-1000CORE. Original observations and historical next-model notes remain below as provenance; current queue is in research-state.json.

<!-- BOSS-GT-1000 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-034 --> model; separate from GT-1000CORE.

### Display and scoped technical checks

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-1000/) establishes **one backlit graphic LCD,512×160 pixels**. It does not provide a diagonal or explicit color-depth/touch specification in the inspected text. Attempts to open the official front/top photos failed, so this pass does **not** claim a successful visual monochrome/touch verification. The color and touch fields retain an explicit evidence gap rather than borrowing GT-1000CORE's screen.

The current page confirms **7 rotary controls,10 main footswitches,5 separate buttons**. Its feature text explicitly identifies the expression **toe switch**, which was not stated in the old hardware row. Current totals are185 effect types and250 factory+250 user memories; older145-type retailer copy is not the current source of truth. The built-in Bluetooth editor supports iOS/Android, alongside Windows/macOS Tone Studio; the product does not require BT-DUAL.

These checks do not finish current category subtotals, exact synth/acoustic algorithm names, USB-bus-power verification or every earlier field. No launch-era effect count is silently promoted to current firmware.

## GT-1000CORE

### GT-1000CORE — current comparison summary

Selected 2026-10-28 from the complete dated evidence below; no new hardware test or market refresh. All older sources and qualifications remain intact.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->
| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Built-in / external expression | No built-in treadle or toe switch; two shared CTL/EXP sockets for direct analog pedals. SYSTEM preference + EXP1/EXP2 FOOT VOL shares the target across patches. Competing patch ASSIGN priority remains unverified. |
| Expression / combined function choices | Indexed original [CORE Parameter Guide, eng01, CONTROL FUNCTION table](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-1000CORE_prm_eng01_W.pdf), checked2026-11-03: EXP1/EXP2 choices are **OFF, FOOT VOL, PEDAL FX, FV/PD FX, FV+TUNER, FVTU/PFX**. PEDAL FX follows the selected wah/pedal-bend effect. FV+TUNER opens the tuner at full heel; FVTU/PFX does so when using its foot-volume role. These are alternative function settings, not six analog inputs, a guaranteed always-active volume role or proof of attenuation during tuner bypass. SYSTEM target sharing remains separately documented; range, competing ASSIGN priority and output coverage stay qualified. Original indexed table only; full current2.02 guide equivalence and hardware behavior unverified. |
| Expression / external toe and calibration limits | Roland exact-model articles, updated **2026-05-20**, checked **2026-11-03**: [toe-end switch assignment through EXP is unsupported](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/360054113912-GT-1000CORE-Can-the-toe-end-of-an-expression-pedal-be-used-as-a-switch-to-turn-WAH-on-off); [neither CTL2,3/EXP1 nor CTL4,5/EXP2 has expression calibration](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/360054113872-GT-1000CORE-Does-it-have-an-expression-pedal-calibration-function). Separate CTL contacts and midpoint-triggered ASSIGNs are different mechanisms. No universal third-party/M-VAVE pinout compatibility inferred. SYSTEM FOOT VOL remains documented, with range/placement/coverage qualifications unchanged. |
| Expression volume placement / range / recall | Movable FV block, including parallel chains; uniform factory placement unknown. MIN/MAX/position 0–100 and four curves are patch parameters; no SYSTEM range override established. EXP HOLD retains angle when the destination function matches; recall is separate from assignment. |
| Expression volume coverage / bypass | Headphone, tail and recorded-loop attenuation unverified. Tuner MUTE/BYPASS/THRU are distinct; no explicit pedal-sweep guarantee in bypass. NS detection at FV OUT versus NS INPUT affects tail behavior separately. |
| Headphones / preset navigation | L/PHONES shares OUTPUT signal, not SUB OUT; leave R/MONO empty for phones. Onboard patch down/up documented. |
| Controls | 7 rotary controls / 3 footswitches / 5 separate buttons. |
| Dimensions / mass | 173 W × 135 D × 65 H mm including rubber feet; 920 g. Fits preferred ≤200 W ×140 D footprint. |
| Display | Graphic LCD 256×80; historically inspected official image shows monochrome-style readout, not a color-depth specification. Published diagonal and touchscreen status unknown. |
| Simultaneous processing / choices | 24 blocks including two amps; current product page lists 185 effect types and eight bass drive types. Full current category census remains unresolved. |
| Preset / user memory | 250 factory preset +250 user locations; initial user filling not established here. |
| Preset / software library | Tone Studio eng05: **300 patches/liveset,30 livesets**; device restore omits251+. These software limits do not expand250 user slots. Liveset.tsl,Stompbox.stx andall-data.alb are distinct backups. [CORE applicability](#gt-1000core--core-applicable-tone-studio-ir-formats-and-library-boundaries). |
| Synth / acoustic | **AC.G SIM acoustic simulation** documented in the original2020 CORE parameter guide,p13;AC RESO separately enhances an acoustic-electric pickup. Dedicated tracking synth/current2.02 algorithm census remain unverified. [Exact-model guide](https://files.kraftmusic.com/media/ownersmanual/Boss_GT-1000CORE_Parameter_Guide.pdf). |
| IR / NAM / capture | **16 user WAV IR slots**. Shared Tone Studio guide, explicitly covering CORE: **44.1/48/96kHz,16/24-bit linear** source files, converted before loading. Length/channel limits and current app equivalence unresolved; native/converted NAM and capture import unestablished. [Original-document applicability and librarian scope](#gt-1000core--core-applicable-tone-studio-ir-formats-and-library-boundaries). |
| Audio / controller I/O | Stereo quarter-inch input/output; L output shared with phones; two mono effects loops; two shared CTL/EXP; 3.5 mm TRS MIDI IN/OUT; micro-USB. |
| Power | 670 mA unit draw. Roland’s 2025-07-15 exact-model adapter table maps GT-1000CORE to PSB-1U: 9 V DC, 2 A adapter capacity; 100–240 V AC, 50/60 Hz input. Capacity is not unit draw. Polarity, barrel dimensions, battery and USB bus operation remain unverified. |
| Editor / USB | BOSS Tone Studio Windows/macOS; drivers separate. USB audio/reamping documented, exact channel/mode limits unresolved. |
| Editor / mobile support boundary | [Roland CORE support](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/360054597651-GT-1000CORE-Can-I-edit-GT-1000CORE-settings-from-a-mobile-device), updated2026-05-20, checked2026-11-03, states no Bluetooth MIDI and no mobile editing/control support. This manufacturer support statement is not a test of every third-party wired host/MIDI workaround or an absence-of-USB-MIDI claim. |
| I/O / SEND setting version floor | [Roland exact-model support](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/360054525211-GT-1000CORE-SUB-OUT-AUX-MUTE-is-not-displayed-in-the-SEND1-2-SETTING) says **SUB OUT,AUX MUTE** in SEND1/2 settings requires **system1.02 or later**. Article updated2026-05-20; checked2026-11-03. This is menu availability, not proof of global expression attenuation of AUX, headphones, tails or recorded loops. |
| Power / version-two idle behavior | [Roland CORE support article](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/27497005138459-GT-1000CORE-After-updating-to-system-program-Ver-2-the-power-turns-off-automatically),updated **2026-05-20**,checked **2026-11-03**: systemVer.2 changes AUTO OFF to **20 minutes of inactivity**. MENU→HARDWARE SETTING→OTHER offers **20MIN,1HOUR,5HOURS,10HOURS,OFF**. This is documented idle shutdown,not evidence ofa supply fault,USB power support orwhich activities reset the timer. Settings andinstalled firmware were not changed ortested. |
| Editor / separate Windows USB driver scope | [Exact CORE support entry](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gt-1000core/updates_drivers/1934779a-9286-4202-9708-8ae69ab716ce/),checked **2026-11-03**,explicitly permits the GT-1000 driver onCORE. **1.1.1** supports Windows11 andWindows10 64-bit;Windows10 **32-bit remains1.1.0**. **ARM PCs,virtual Windows,Windows onMac andWindows10 Mobile are unsupported**. These are driver requirements,not native ARM emulation guarantees,Tone Studio editor minima orhardware firmware numbers. No installer downloaded orrun. |
| I/O / historical USB mix boundary | [Original2020 CORE parameter guide,p46](https://files.kraftmusic.com/media/ownersmanual/Boss_GT-1000CORE_Parameter_Guide.pdf),checked2026-11-03: computer MIX LEVEL **0–200%** is mixed at the final OUTPUT or SUB OUT stage. EFX OUT controls processed recording level;DRY OUT is unprocessed andTO EFX feeds computer audio through effects. These separate routes do not establish universal FV-block attenuation. DIR MON is not stored andreturnsON atstartup. SUB OUT DIR MON prose also mentions PHONES,conflicting with the owner's OUTPUT-only headphone statement;do not infer SUB playback inphones. Current2.02 equivalence remains unverified. |
| Looper / routing | 38 s mono /19 s stereo; audio lost when looper is turned off or power removed. P/U246–250 initially map CTL1 to looper; other patches need assignment or SYSTEM sharing. Serial/parallel routing, 96 kHz and qualified carryover documented. |
| Firmware / documentation | Official support lists system 2.02 and Tone Studio 4.1.1. July2026 2.02 notes concern PLAY OPTION and switching fixes. Readable eng12 MIDI map header covers CORE2.01, with older chart metadata; not 2.02 certification. Owner eng03 mirror is not the eng03 Parameter Guide; exact current guide mapping unresolved. |
| Used IDR | Unknown; no exact condition-verified Indonesian used offer established. |
| Shopee retail-style IDR | Boss Official Shop, Tangerang Selatan, [341882414 /15035629131](https://shopee.co.id/BOSS-GT-1000CORE-Guitar-Effects-Processor-Pedal-Multi-Efek-i.341882414.15035629131): Rp14.450.000 /Habis in three-month-old snapshot reopened2026-10-28. Crossed Rp17.000.000, advertised15% reduction and unspecified free gift. Condition grade, bundle/adapter, affiliation, warranty, checkout stock and delivered price unconfirmed; late-delivery voucher is not a checkout discount. |
| Separate non-Shopee IDR asks | GMI Rp14.535.000 available label and older Blibli Rp13.600.000 indexed stock1 retain their original dates/channel limits below; not live Shopee stock or a used/new range. |
| Synth / pitch / instrument simulation / special | Dedicated tracking synth unverified. OVERTONE adds harmonics; HUMANIZR vowel processing; RING MOD oscillator modulation; RESO WAH resonant filtering. PITCH SFT ±24 semitones: MONO mode single-note; other modes input polyphony unverified. OCTAVE MONO versus explicitly polyphonic one-octave-down POLY. HARMONST and PDL BEND require single notes; S-BEND sweeps pitch. AC.G SIM, SITAR SIM, DEFRETR and DEFRET B; AC RESO processes acoustic-pickup sound, rather than establishing electric-to-acoustic simulation. SLICER, SND HOLD (single-note), FEEDBAKR, SLW GEAR; GLITCH in MASTER DELAY. [Revision and restrictions](#gt-1000core--creative-effects-capability-check-2026-11-03). |

### Display

- **One graphic LCD,256×80 pixels**, manufacturer specification. Published diagonal was not located and remains **unknown**, not estimated from the chassis.
- The [official product photograph](https://static.roland.com/products/gt-1000core/images/gt-1000core_top_gal.jpg) was successfully opened and visually inspected: **monochrome-style readout**. Colored footswitch LEDs are not a color graphical display. The manufacturer does not separately state the screen's color depth; this is visual evidence, not a panel-datasheet claim.
- **Touchscreen: unverified/no touch interface documented**. Color/monochrome appearance and knob navigation alone are not definitive touch-hardware specifications.

The [manufacturer product/specification page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-1000core/) also confirms **7 knobs,3 footswitches,5 buttons**,24 simultaneous blocks,185 effect types,250 factory+250 user locations and16 IR slots. It lists system program2.02 and Tone Studio4.1.1; this is a publication observation, not the owner's installed version. Complete current category counts and synth/acoustic algorithms remain open.

### GT-1000CORE — system expression-volume assignment, 2026-10-19

[Manufacturer-authored owner manual mirror](https://www.notice-facile.com/en/manual/1420090/boss%2Bgt-1000core), exact GT-1000CORE original body pp2,4,13: **PREF=SYSTEM shares expression functions across all patches**. [Own parameter guide mirror](https://manualzz.com/doc/90259078/boss-gt-1000core-guitar-effects-processor-owner-manual), original CONTROL FUNCTION table, offers EXP1/EXP2 FUNCTION=FOOT VOL. Thus system-wide target assignment is documented, not merely calibration/position recall. No device test; competing patch ASSIGN interactions remain unchecked.

[Official parameter guide eng03](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-1000CORE_parameter_eng03_W.pdf), indexed table: volume minimum/maximum0–100; curves Slow1/Slow2/Normal/Fast. Their global scope and uniform FV placement across factory presets remain unverified. Bypass, tails and looper coverage need signal-path review. Headphones share L/PHONES: leave R/MONO empty; only OUTPUT signal, not SUB OUT, reaches phones. Onboard down/up selects patches. Existing173×135mm chassis fits preference. [Capability comparison](../EXPRESSION-VOLUME.md).

Official owner/parameter PDF reopening returned403; mirrors' original body and indexed official tables used, not generated diagrams or FAQs. No downloads or device operation.

### GT-1000CORE — tuner bypass versus through sound

Checked2026-10-28 in [original BOSS parameter guide mirror](https://files.kraftmusic.com/media/ownersmanual/Boss_GT-1000CORE_Parameter_Guide.pdf),57pages,©2020Roland,printedp55. Tuner **MUTE** suppresses output; **BYPASS** passes the incoming guitar unchanged with all effects off; **THRU** retains the current effect sound. These are tuner modes,not three generic descriptions of normal operation. Foot-volume sweep during BYPASS versus THRU is not explicitly established; SYSTEM target sharing cannot be promoted to all-mode attenuation from these descriptions. This older guide still lists a polyphonic tuner removed in2.01,so it is not certified as current2.02 documentation. Existing firmware qualifications,patch-stored range/routing andheadphone/tail/recorded-loop gaps remain. No device test ordownload.

### GT-1000CORE — Mac editor, driver and OS compatibility boundaries

Checked **2026-11-05**. [BOSS compatibility table,last updated30October2026](https://www.boss.info/global/support/support_news/25090811m1/),exact GT-1000CORE row: **macOS15 Sequoia and26 Tahoe: Yes(Download)** for both **Hardware & Driver** and **Exclusive Application**; **macOS27 Golden Gate: Under Testing** for both. This is a dated manufacturer status,not a promise for every later OS build orproof of universal class compliance. CPU-architecture details are not established by this table.

The [exact CORE Mac editor entry](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gt-1000core/updates_drivers/ded9b650-2054-472a-87ed-a49c0a1a1ff6/) lists **ToneStudio4.1.1,March2026**,compatible with **CORE2.01 orlater**,and requires a separate driver. Fixes concern mismatched CTRL ASSIGN AMP TYPE ordering and IR Loader failures with particular MIDI DEVICE ID values. Version4.1.0,February2025,added direct Tone Exchange and IR Loader access. These editor changes do not add a hardware synth orprove global FV behavior.

The [separate Mac USBdriver1.0.3 entry](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gt-1000core/updates_drivers/f3987563-ca8d-4c4b-8fbc-5d9999d3b9d8/) is headed Sonoma14.x orlater but directs current OS status to the compatibility table;the heading mustnot override its macOS27 testing status. Driver,editor andCORE system2.02 have separate version series. No download,installation ordevice test;installed versions,ARM-specific requirements,global expression coverage andqualified Shopee stock remain unresolved.

## GT-Pro

Current comparison summary, reviewed 2026-10-28. Discontinued rack guitar multi-effects; ownership and bass/vocal suitability remain unverified. Detailed dated sources, earlier summaries and contradictions are retained below. The comparison uses this summary rather than concatenating every historical parameter table.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->
| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Display / contrast adjustment | Original manual p96: SYSTEM→LCD Contrast offers **1–16**, for the right-hand LCD. This is a contrast parameter,not a diagonal,color-depth,touch or LED-brightness specification. [Indexed manufacturer original](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf),checked2026-11-03. |
| Knobs / pickup behavior | Original p99: **Immediate** changes the value as soon as a knob moves; **Current Setting** waits until its physical position reaches the stored patch value. This is front-panel knob pickup,not expression-pedal Assign Hold or a global volume-range override. [Indexed manufacturer original](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf),checked2026-11-03. |
| Expression | No built-in treadle. EXP1 and shared EXP2/CTL3,4 accept external control; CTL1,2 is separate. EV-5/FV-300L specified; set pedal minimum to MIN. Global SYSTEM **Foot Volume and Patch Level** roles documented;Patch Level is a separate direct target,not a per-patch ASSIGN. Foot Volume precedence over patch FV enable/MIN/MAX remains unknown. Auto can switch to wah/bend; it is not a volume lock. External toe capability unverified. |
| Knobs / footswitches / buttons | **12 / 0 / 29**, reconciled from original manual pp11–12; [explicit count and prior30 correction](#gt-pro--reconciled-textual-control-census). POWER and rear ground-lift separate; panel-image corroboration pending. No onboard preset footswitch down/up. |
| Display | Backlit **16×2-character LCD** plus **2-character seven-segment LED**; corroborated by the exact GT-PRO specification body in [BOSS Products Catalog 2008, PDF p21](https://images.static-thomann.de/pics/atg/atgdata/document/brochure/189895_broschure.pdf). Diagonal, color and touch remain unverified. |
| Dimensions / mass | **482 W × 305 D × 89 H mm; 4.8 kg**. Far exceeds preferred 200 W × 140 D mm. |
| Processing / categories | **44 catalogue entries: 22 shared FX families + 11 FX2-only + 11 other entries**, including external LOOP and FV. FX1 selects one of 22 families; FX2 one of 33. Catalogue and original manual p10 claim15 simultaneous effects; Japanese specifications also say13. [Primary-manual qualification](#gt-pro--owner-manual-count-claim-and-windows-driver-endpoints-source-details); counting basis unresolved. |
| Live / patch tempo and MIDI clock | [Original manual pp55,74](#gt-pro--patch-tempo-and-midi-clock-priority): Master BPM40–250 is patch-specific; Auto follows incoming clock,otherwise patch BPM; Internal uses patch BPM. No cross-patch tempo hold orclock-transition continuity established. |
| Processing / menu choices | **46 named preamps + 3 custom definitions**, two amp channels combinable. OD/DS **30+3 custom**; WAH **6+3 custom**; dedicated COMP **2 modes**; DELAY **12 types**; REVERB **7**; CHORUS **3 modes**. FX-family subtypes and full lists are in the detailed record; these are not additional simultaneous slots. |
| Memory | **200 writable User + 200 protected Preset = 400**. Original patch lists verify **200 named User entries, U1-1 through U20-10**: English pp152–154 (150) plus German p155 (50). This establishes documented initial User-bank filling, not current used-unit contents or 400 protected factory presets. Shared custom definitions affect all referencing patches. |
| Memory / backup battery | [Original manual warning](#gt-pro--memory-backup-battery-and-service-boundary): internal battery preserves memory with main power off; Battery Low! appears at startup. This is not audio-operation battery power; replacement type/life and current condition remain unknown. |
| Memory / selective factory restoration | Original GT-PRO manual p103 and Roland support article updated2024-10-22 distinguish **System** (system parameters, Harmonist scales, Auto Riff phrases and custom preamp/speaker/drive/wah definitions), **Quick** (User Quick Settings), and **user patches1-1–20-10**. Full initialization spans System→20-10. This is restoration of the selected data range, not extra memory or proof of current unit contents; back up edited data first. [Exact source details](#gt-pro--selective-factory-restoration-and-shared-data-scope). |
| Synth / acoustic | Guitar Synth, Wave Synth, acoustic/sitar simulation and Acoustic Processor documented. Harmonist and Intelligent Ring Modulator require single notes; Pitch Shifter supports chords except Mono mode. No blanket polyphony claim or special-pickup requirement inferred. |
| I/O / global input correction | [Original manufacturer manual,printedp97](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf),indexed text checked2026-11-04: **Input Level andInput Presence each−20…+20dB**,affecting every patch. Presence adjusts theguitar high-end tone;neither isa selectable amplifier Presence knob. The manual warns these changes can alter guitar-volume-responsive effect behavior. This is global input conditioning,not analog EXP/FV assignment orfinal-output attenuation;no hardware changed. |
| I/O / converters and clock | **A/D24-bit + Adaptive Focus;D/A24-bit;44.1kHz**. Coaxial formatEIAJ CP1201/S/PDIF. These do not replace Standard/Special USB stream formats orcertify measured dynamic range. [Exact manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/hu/products/gt-pro/),checked2026-11-03. |
| Audio I/O | Front/rear guitar inputs, front priority; dedicated PHONES and DIRECT/TUNER out. MAIN L/R and SUB quarter-inch L/R are unbalanced; SUB XLR L/R balanced and fixed +4 dBu, unaffected by SUB knob/OUT Level. Coaxial 24-bit S/PDIF. Nominal levels/impedances retained below; headphone power/load unverified. |
| I/O / headphone mix and input peak | Original manualpp11,17 explicitly make **PHONES a mix of MAIN andSUB**;p17 directs using **both MAIN andSUB knobs** toadjust headphone level. This is headphone monitoring,not independently exposed USB channels orproof all routes follow globalFV. **PEAK lights6dB before input distortion**;INPUT LEVEL adjusts front/rear input signals. The indicator margin is not a maximum input-voltage rating ormeasured headroom test. [Indexed manufacturer original](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf),checked2026-11-03;fullPDF exceeds reader size limit. [Matching originalp17 reproduction](https://www.manualshelf.com/manual/roland/gt-pro/owner-s-manual-english/page-17.html). |
| I/O / output-device processing | [Original pp17–18](#gt-pro--output-device-choices-and-speaker-simulation-boundary): separate MAIN/SUB Output Select, each with eight device choices. Speaker simulation requires **LINE/PHONES**; these selections do not change tone with PREAMP/SPEAKER Off. Distinct from output routing,USB channels andglobal FV. |
| Routing / loops | PRE LOOP fixed before input level and unavailable with USB In. LOOP1/2 can combine stereo or operate series/parallel, but cannot occupy separate chain positions. MAIN/SUB source selection and digital-output tap documented. FV is movable; downstream Synth/Feedbacker OSC can defeat intended volume response. |
| Routing / stereo send distinction | **Stereo1: stereo send/return; Stereo2: mono send/stereo return.** Branch Out ignores returns; the [manufacturer wet/dry recipe](#gt-pro--stereo-send-modes-and-wetdry-branch-placement) taps before spatial effects. Not inherently a raw DI or universal FV coverage. |
| Controls / external navigation | [Original external-control functions](#gt-pro--external-patch-navigation-and-level-steps): Patch Num. Inc/Dec stay within the current bank; separate bank commands. No onboard preset footswitches. Patch-level steps are not continuous expression assignment. |
| Data / control | USB, MIDI IN/OUT/THRU; AMP CTRL1/2; EXP1, shared EXP2/CTL3,4, CTL1,2. USB control/editor requires Advanced mode; rear DIN MIDI cannot act as a computer MIDI interface. |
| Power | Regional **AC 117/230/240 V; 16 W consumption**; [Japanese specification](https://www.boss.info/jp/products/gt-pro/support/) separately lists **AC100 V; 15 W** (reopened2026-10-28). These are regional ratings, not universal-voltage capability; check the exact unit. Battery and USB-powered operation unverified; do not infer bus power from USB audio. |
| USB audio | [USB 1.0/1.1 operation even on USB 2.0 ports](#gt-pro--fc-300-integration-versus-direct-analog-expression); newer port does not increase performance. **44.1 kHz**; Special **24-bit**, Standard **16-bit**. Historical Mac OS9 ASIO explicitly has **one stereo input and one stereo output**; other driver-mode channel counts and current hosts unverified. MAIN+SUB is a mix, not four USB channels. Dry tap/reamping documented; software Thru must be disabled for USB In. |
| USB / historical Windows driver roles | Original p123 lists WDM/ASIO audio as BOSS GT-PRO, MME input/output separately named, and BOSS GT-PRO Control for MIDI/SysEx. These endpoint names do not enumerate audio channels or extend modern-host support. [Source details](#gt-pro--owner-manual-count-claim-and-windows-driver-endpoints-source-details). |
| USB / historical Mac host-volume boundary | [Original manual p137](#gt-pro--standard-driver-host-volume-boundary): Mac OS X Standard-driver Main Volume slider is unavailable. This is historical host behavior,not an EXP volume lock or current-host compatibility. |
| USB / historical latency and monitoring limits | [Original manufacturer manual, indexed p141](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf), checked **2026-11-01**: Advanced mode permits driver-buffer adjustment; the minimum slider position favors low latency, while larger buffers address interruptions. The audio application must be restarted after changes. No numeric round-trip latency or current-host performance is established. **ASIO Direct Monitor may switch on/off unexpectedly**, depending on application settings and recording procedure; the manual’s remedy is disabling its driver checkbox. This is host-controlled monitoring behavior, not a global expression-volume override or additional USB channels. No driver installed or device tested. [Direct-monitor output and command scope](#gt-pro--direct-monitor-switch-versus-volume-control) is separate from pedal volume. |
| Editor / platforms | Historical Windows/Mac editor/librarian and separate drivers. The [2008 catalogue requirements](#gt-pro--2008-catalogue-editor-requirements-and-display-identity) explicitly include Vista and Mac G3/600MHz,256MB RAM,200MB disk; dated requirements differ from the older listing. Mac editor evidence includes 10.2.3–10.6.8, explicitly not 10.7+; Mountain Lion driver support does not establish editor support. **Separate USB Driver1.0.1 for Mavericks10.9 is also listed**;[exact listing and limitations](#gt-pro--mavericks-driver-listing-versus-legacy-editor). BOSS marks Windows10/11 hardware/driver and application incompatible (Aug2026). **Yosemite10.10:manufacturer marks hardware/driver andbundled app No**;[exact historical boundary](#gt-pro--yosemite-hardware-and-application-status). Current macOS/Linux unverified. |
| IR / NAM / capture | Import support unverified. Two parameter-based custom speaker definitions are not evidence of IR/NAM/capture loading. |
| Practical / Japanese repair status | **BOSS Japan, October2026: repair acceptance ended (×)**. Exact GT-PRO row lists release2005, production completion2010 and six-year performance-parts retention period; not a worldwide service ruling or exact repair-end date. [Regional source qualification](#gt-pro--japanese-manufacturer-repair-acceptance-status). |
| Synth / Auto Riff phrase editing | Original p49: **IN** selects the input note, **STEP** the phrase step, **OUT** its output note. Guitar notes can supply input-note detection andadvance the step. A **tie carries the preceding note**; **end makes the preceding step the final step**. These are programmed note sequences,not stored audio,additional synth polyphony orextra patch memories. Existing30 prepared/10 User phrase counts andsingle-note input restriction remain. [Indexed manufacturer original](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf),checked2026-11-04. |

Current mode retains the documented pitch-detection-effect warning;global FV bypass qualifications remain separate. [Matching original English reproduction](https://www.manualshelf.com/manual/roland/gt-pro/owner-s-manual-english/page-102.html),checked2026-11-04. |

Patch Extent limits selectable addresses through a configurable upper bound1-1…40-10;FC-200 mode shifts displayed banks down one. [Exact scope](#gt-pro--global-corrections-and-selectable-patch-range). |

### GT-Pro — exact loop pairing and USB-input restriction

Checked 2026-10-19 against manufacturer-authored GT-PRO manual reproduced by ManualsLib, printed [p52](https://www.manualslib.com/manual/499099/Boss-Gt-Pro.html?page=52) and [p53](https://www.manualslib.com/manual/499099/Boss-Gt-Pro.html?page=53). Exact title,164-page structure andmatching LOOP/PRELOOP sections corroborate original manual identity;original body only,not generated Q&A.

| Field | Manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / stereo-loop pairing | LOOP1+2 use two send/return pairs together: **Stereo1 stereo-send/stereo-return;Stereo2 mono-send/stereo-return**. They cannot occupy different effect-chain positions. Alternatively1→2 is series and1&2 parallel. This qualifies overview wording;not three independently stereo loops. |
| I/O / loop modes | Normal inserts external processing;DirectMix blends return with loop-input dry signal;BranchOut sends outward while ignoring return. Send/return levels0–200 are relative controls,not dBu ratings. |
| I/O / PRE LOOP restriction | Fixed ahead ofINPUTLEVEL;cannot reorder. **Unavailable with InputSelect=USBIn**. Distortion placed here can impair pitch-detection effects such as guitar synth/Harmonist. This is an actual USB reprocessing restriction,not an extra USB channel. |

Earlier general loop ambiguity is superseded by these explicit manual routing details. USB stream format,control totals,memory protection andcomplete category census remain unresolved. No download orhardware operation.

### GT-Pro — pitch-shifter modes and Auto Riff memory

Checked 2026-10-19 against the [official original manual p46](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf), [original p48 mirror](https://roland.manymanuals.es/supplementary-music-equipment/gt-pro/owners-manual-5239/48), [original p49 mirror](https://www.manualshelf.com/manual/roland/gt-pro/owner-s-manual-english/page-49.html) and [Spanish manual p48](https://www.manualslib.es/manual/12527/Boss-Gt-Pro.html?page=48). Indexed manufacturer-authored GT-PRO tables; exact model and pagination match. No device test.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / Pitch Shifter | **3 voice modes:** 1-Voice, 2-Mono, 2-Stereo. **4 processing modes:** Fast, Medium, Slow, Mono. Normal pitch shifting accepts chords; Mono is for single notes. Fast→Medium→Slow trades slower response for reduced modulation. Pitch ±24 semitones; two-voice modes allow separate PS1/PS2 settings. Voice modes and processing modes are separate parameters, not seven additional effect families. |
| Memory / Auto Riff | **30 prepared phrases + 10 user phrases**. These are Auto Riff phrase definitions, not additional patch memories or audio-loop recordings. Phrases are triggered by picking a single note; chords are unsupported. Heavy incoming MIDI traffic may disturb playback. |
| Live / Auto Riff controls | Loop On repeats the phrase; Hold sustains the effect after input ends. Trigger sensitivity controls restarting; zero prevents retriggering. Key selection applies to prepared phrases. These controls do not establish a drum machine or persistent audio looper. |

Remaining FX subtypes, 44-category reconciliation, USB channel count and qualified IDR hardware offers remain unresolved. No downloads or device operations.

### GT-Pro — ring modulation and vowel-mode distinctions, 2026-10-28

Indexed manufacturer-authored GT-PRO manual body, [p41](https://www.manualshelf.com/manual/roland/gt-pro/owner-s-manual-english/page-41.html) and [p43](https://www.manualshelf.com/manual/roland/gt-pro/owner-s-manual-english/page-43.html);same exact manual/model/pagination as prior checks. P43 direct reopening returned cache miss;indexed original table used,not generated FAQs. No new download or device test.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / Ring Modulator | **2 modes:Normal,Intelligent**. Intelligent follows detected input pitch and requires single notes rather than chords;Normal uses the internal oscillator without that pitch-following behavior. Frequency0–100 is relative,not a published Hz range. These are modes within sharedFX RM,not extra simultaneous blocks. |
| Processing / Humanizer | **3 modes:Picking,Auto,Random**. Picking transitions Vowel1→Vowel2 with picking;Auto cycles the selected pair;Random selects among **a,e,i,o,u**. Five vowels are choices within the effect,not five additional families. Rate/depth and mode-dependent sensitivity do not add algorithms. |
| Processing / Anti-feedback | Up to **three fixed cancellation frequencies**,each with independent depth. Frequency0–100 is a control range,not Hz or a three-family count. This suppresses guitar-body resonance;it is separate from the Feedbacker effect. |

### GT-Pro — Assign Hold position recall (2026-10-28)

Manufacturer-authored original **GT-PRO manual p99**, indexed [English PDF reproduction](https://c3.zzounds.com/media/GT-PRO_e2-c15bef3b6baf301921f986b46ca7c501.pdf), corroborated by [same English page](https://www.manualshelf.com/manual/roland/gt-pro/owner-s-manual-english/page-99.html) and [Spanish manufacturer manual p99](https://www.manualslib.es/manual/12527/Boss-Gt-Pro.html?page=99). Exact model, chapter and pagination match earlier original-manual checks. Indexed original body only: direct PDF exceeds reader size limit and English HTML reopening missed cache; no visual/device test or download.

| Setting | Documented recall behavior / limitation |
|---|---|
| Assign Hold On | Carries the pedal-assignment state across patch changes. Volume-to-volume example follows the pedal’s current angle. If the destination assigns wah instead, its volume takes the patch value and wah follows the pedal angle. This is position/state retention, **not a global target lock**. |
| Assign Hold Off | Destination volume initially uses its saved patch value; subsequent pedal movement/information changes it according to the pedal. No uninterrupted held-position volume guarantee. |
| Toggle source | Assign Hold does not operate when Assign Source mode is Toggle. This restriction is distinct from a continuous analog pedal’s calibration. |

This resolves the documented ASSIGN recall examples, not SYSTEM fixed FootVolume versus dedicated FV enable/MIN/MAX precedence or EXP2 memo ambiguity. The earlier competing-source warning remains applicable. EXP1/FC-200 tuner-bypass volume remains documented;other bypass states,phones,tails/recorded-Hold coverage remain unknown.

### GT-Pro — noise-suppressor detector and volume-tail interaction

### GT-Pro — stereo send modes and wet/dry branch placement

Checked **2026-11-04**, indexed original [manufacturer owner manual, printedp52](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf) and [exact-model BOSS wet/dry support example](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404525989915), updated2022-01-18. Full PDF exceeds reader size limit; original indexed text was read. No device operation or local download.

**Stereo1 sends and returns stereo; Stereo2 sends mono and returns stereo.** These are different loop topologies, not proof that every upstream effect is stereo. The paired loops still cannot occupy independent chain positions. Branch Out sends the signal at the loop position and ignores returns; it is not inherently an untouched guitar DI.

The support example places the branch **before reverb/delay**, sending that earlier signal to one amp and setting direct sound to zero on the later main-output effects for one or two wet amps. This is a two/three-amp routing recipe, not additional internal effects. The English manual’s example instead uses ambiguous wording about reverb/delay being immediately ahead of the loop; retain this source wording difference rather than treating the English phrase as proof of a dry tap after those effects. The support recipe makes the intended pre-spatial-effect tap explicit.

Volume attenuation depends on routing/FV placement; this example does not resolve global-FV enable/range precedence or headphone/USB/tail coverage. Fresh exact Shopee Indonesia searching yielded unrelated phone/disc products, not a qualified GT-Pro hardware offer; no price or stock inferred. Preserve current13/15 effect-count, USB-mode and physical-control gaps.

No onboard footswitches;external controller sockets do not increase thephysical count. ### GT-Pro — virtual-pedal modulation and target exclusions

Checked **2026-11-04**, indexed original [manufacturer owner manual,printedpp70–71](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf). These are patch ASSIGN sources,not physical pedals oradditional audio-effect blocks;ASSIGN Source Mode must be **Normal**.

**Internal Pedal** starts a sweep from a selected trigger,including patch selection,external pedals orspecified MIDI CCs. Time **0–100** has no seconds conversion established here;curves are **Linear,Slow Rise,Fast Rise**. **Wave Pedal** cycles with **SAW,TRI,SIN**,Rate **0–100**;this is not a documented Hz/BPM range. It cannot control **FX1/FX2 Select** or **Type forPreamp/Speaker,Overdrive/Distortion,Delay**.

The manual warns that assigning either virtual source to tuner/manual on-off orpatch-number/bank increment/decrement can prevent parameter editing;it describes overwriting/saving as the recovery. No such assignment was made. Automatic sweeps/cycles are distinct from tracked synthesis anddirect analog global-volume assignment;fixedSYSTEM FVrange/coverage remains unresolved. No download ordevice operation.

### GT-Pro — dated Japanese service lifecycle

Checked **2026-11-04** against [BOSS Japan’s model-specific repair/support table](https://www.boss.info/jp/support/service_repair/1708080000/),explicitly current **October2026**. Its exact **GT-PRO** row lists **launch2005,production completion2010**,andrepair status **×**. The page defines×as **factory repair intake ended**,not merely awaiting parts. The row’s **six-year performance-parts retention period** is a service-policy field afterproduction completion,not a new six-year warranty orparts-stock guarantee.

This qualifies the discontinued-model record with a dated manufacturer source. It doesnot prove Indonesian service arrangements,third-party repair impossibility,used-unit condition orthe availability ofaparticular replacement part. No repair quote,service request orseller contact wasmade. The listed elapsed time is not extrapolated beyond the table’s date.

ExactShopeeIndonesia search still yielded no qualified GT-Pro hardware offer. GlobalFV precedence,other USB-mode channel counts,13/15effect-count reconciliation andpanel-image corroboration remain unresolved. Continue other accessible priority-model work whilepreserving this independent general cursor;no download ordevice operation.

### GT-Pro — patch tempo and MIDI-clock priority

Checked **2026-11-05** against indexed original [manufacturer owner manual,printedpp55,74](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf),©2005 BOSS. Full PDF exceeds the reader limit; original page text was readable,not a visual diagram ordevice inspection.

**Master BPM40–250 is set per patch**. MIDI Sync Clock has **Auto** and **Internal** options: Auto uses the patch BPM without received external MIDI clock, and follows external clock while received; Internal uses Master BPM. The manual says selecting Internal restores manual BPM editing when external synchronization has disabled it,and warns incoming-clock errors can cause timing problems. A connected device alone doesnot establish that it is sending clock.

An external footswitch can provide Master BPM tap through ASSIGN. This doesnot establish onboard tap footswitches,global tempo hold across patch changes,USB-versus-DIN priority orseamless transition when clock arrives/disappears. The13-versus15 effects discrepancy,global FVprecedence andother USB-mode channel counts remain unresolved. No MIDI transmitted,settings changed,local download ordevice operation.

### GT-Pro — memory backup battery and service boundary

Checked **2026-11-05**, indexed original [manufacturer GT-PRO Owner’s Manual,printedp5,Memory Backup andRepairs andData](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf);same exact-model troubleshooting passage is indexed with the sound checks onprintedp143. Full PDF exceeds reader limits;no local download.

The unit contains a battery powering memory circuits while main power isoff. **Battery Low!** appears at startup when it weakens;the manufacturer warns ofpossible loss ofall memory data andrefers replacement to dealer/service personnel. It also warns repair can erase memory and recommends an external MIDI/computer backup. This establishes memory retention hardware,not battery-powered audio operation,USB bus power,a guaranteed service life ora verified replacement cell type.

The manual’s historical service referral doesnot override the separately recorded **October2026 BOSS Japan repair-acceptance end** or establish current Indonesian service availability. Exact unit condition,backup integrity andreplacement-data retention remain unknown. No case opened,battery replaced,backup attempted ordevice operated. GlobalFV precedence,USB-mode counts andqualified Shopee offers remain unresolved.

## GX-1

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. This is GX-1, not GX-1B. Full original facts and observation dates remain below. Historical next-model notes are provenance; current work is in research-state.json.

<!-- BOSS-GX-1 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing model in [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-022 -->; not an additional catalogue entry and not GX-1B.

### Display and directly rechecked hardware

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-1/) confirms **one color graphic LCD,320×240 pixels**. A published diagonal was not located. **Touchscreen remains unverified/no touch interface documented**; references to touching the separate effect-category buttons do not prove touchscreen hardware.

The enumerated controls confirm **3 knobs,3 main footswitches,14 separate buttons**, plus expression/toe switching. There are99 user+99 factory memories and146 effect types. Eight simultaneous effects plus volume/noise suppression are distinct from the total model library.

Power is explicitly **fourAA batteries,PSA adapter or USB bus**, with250mA DC/battery and400mA USB draw. The official [GX-1 support page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-1/support/) confirms Windows/macOS/iOS/Android Tone Studio, built-in Bluetooth and WAV IR loading. Those checks do not complete the remaining algorithm/IR-capacity fields.

EFX OUT is processed sound tocomputer;MIX LEVEL returns computer audio at the chain final stage;DRY OUT sends unprocessed guitar;DRY TO EFX feeds computer audio into effects. These controls establish routing,not channel enumeration orstream bit depth. |
| USB / monitor and loopback distinction | LOOPBACK sends the computer/effect mix back tocomputer. DIRECT MONITOR OFF requires computer through-monitoring tohear the guitar;only USB return is otherwise heard. Direct-monitor state is unsaved andreturnsON atstartup. |

| Required field | Current evidence / limits (2026-10-16) |

| Physical controls / expression | Three knobs,three footswitches,fourteen buttons;assignable built-in expression/toe switch. Shared TRS CTL2,3/EXP2 takes up to two switches or external expression. EV-1-WL/FS-1-WL wireless control supported. [GX1B-S] |
| Display / body | Color graphic LCD320×240;diagonal/touch unspecified.307×149×56mm,max73mm;1.2kg,battery-inclusion basis unspecified on inspected specs. [GX1B-S] |
| Processing / memory | Eight simultaneous blocks plus volume/noise suppression;16 AIRD amp types,135 effect types;99 preset+99 user=198 memories. [GX1B-S] |
| Ports / power | Quarter-inch input,L/MONO/R outs;mini phones;TRS CTL/EXP;USB-C;DC.250mA DC/battery,400mA USB;four alkaline AA about5h;PSA-S optional. [GX1B-S] USB supply5V/500mA minimum;USB takes priority over batteries. INPUT plug controls power;ground terminal present. [GX1B-C] |
| Synth / acoustic / category choices | Forty named FX families counted in parameter list;OVERTONE only FX3,so39 available in FX1/2 and40 in FX3. Same eligible algorithm may occupy all three. AC BASS simulates acoustic bass;Bass Synth needs single notes with other strings muted. Remaining subtype/category totals unverified. [GX1B-F] |

Practice metronome/rhythms described within Tone Studio;do not assume a standalone hardware drum machine. [GX1B-S] Drivers,exact USB channels/bitdepth and current OS versions need separate verification. |
| Looper / carryover | 38s mono/19s stereo. [GX1B-S] Carryover requires same effects,types and order in both memories;tails respond to destination settings. This is not unrestricted spillover between arbitrary presets. [GX1B-T] |

### GX-1B — USB driver modes and routing

Checked 2026-10-18 against the model-specific parameter and reference manuals.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / USB driver modes | **VENDOR** is specified for Windows/macOS and requires the GX-1B driver. **GENERIC** uses standard iOS functionality, with some functions potentially limited versus VENDOR. This does not establish generic Windows/macOS support or unrestricted Android compatibility. | [USB parameters](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1b_parameter/en-US/392593931399205515.html) |
| I/O / dry and processed USB routing | EFX OUT LVL controls processed output; DRY OUT sends unprocessed bass. DRY TO EFX controls computer input to the effects, while MIX LEVEL places computer return at the final chain stage. Each has a 0–200% range. This documents dry recording/reprocessing routes; exact stream channels and bit depth remain unverified. | [USB parameters](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1b_parameter/en-US/392593931399205515.html) |
| I/O / monitoring and loopback | Direct monitor switches the unit sound to OUTPUT/PHONES and resets ON at startup; the setting cannot be saved. LOOPBACK sends computer audio mixed with processed bass back to the computer. | [USB parameters](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1b_parameter/en-US/392593931399205515.html) |
| Editor / backup scope | Reference manual explicitly includes backup and restoration of both memories and system settings, alongside memory naming, ordering and sharing. | [Computer connection](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1b_reference/en-US/385298827387664523.html) |

## GX-10

Consolidated and refreshed2026-10-16;partial technical verification. Original dates/contradictions retained. Historical next-model notes below are provenance;current queue is in research-state.json.

<!-- BOSS-GX-10 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing model in [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-014 -->, not an additional catalogue entry.

### Display and directly rechecked specifications

The [manufacturer page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-10/) explicitly specifies **one color graphic LCD,480×272 pixels, with touchscreen**. **Diagonal remains unknown**: a numeric size was not located in the checked primary text and is not inferred from the chassis or retailer wording. The [manufacturer getting-started article](https://articles.boss.info/getting-started-with-the-boss-gx-10/) confirms actual finger-based editing and says the display is the same design introduced on GX-100.

The current product specification confirms **6 knobs,3 footswitches,5 buttons**, plus expression/toe and power separately;15 assignable blocks;32 amps split23 guitar/9 bass;170 effects. Windows/macOS editing and **iOS/Android editing with optional BT-DUAL** remain distinct from ordinary USB mobile recording. This scoped review does not independently certify the complete effect-category inventory or every earlier power/loading field.

### GX-10 — original USD and technical provenance

Research date **2026-10-14**. Technical sources are the linked manufacturer's specifications, including current firmware-dependent figures. **Partial records**: `?` means unverified. Dimensions are **W × D × H in mm**; `/max` gives the maximum height with the pedal raised. `K/F/B` = rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons, excluding the expression toe switch and rear power/mode switches. Price columns are **USD US-market listed/asking observations**, not statistically estimated fair values; see sources below.

Model totals have different inclusion rules. ME-90's selectable alternatives are not all simultaneously present on its panel. A missing NAM claim is not converted into a definitive “No” without model/firmware documentation.

Price retrieval date: **2026-10-14**. Indexed older listings are explicitly less reliable than current, purchasable offers. No guarantee of stock, local Indonesian pricing, delivery cost or completed-sale value.

## GX-100

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. Full original observations, dates and qualifications remain below. Historical next-model notes are provenance; current queue is in research-state.json.

<!-- BOSS-GX-100 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-030 --> record, not another model.

### Display and firmware-sensitive correction

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-100/) confirms a **color graphic LCD,480×272 pixels, with touchscreen**. A numeric diagonal was not found in the checked primary source. Retailers/reviews commonly claim4.3 inches, but this pass does not promote that to manufacturer-verified diagonal evidence.

The official page now advertises **32 AIRD amps**, rather than the launch23. Its version2.0 section explains three added guitar and six added bass amps, seven new effects and eight new factory presets. Current specification still lists170 effect types and100 preset+200 user memories; overlapping marketing counts are not summed. The control list rechecks **6 knobs,8 footswitches,7 buttons**, plus expression toe/power and other rear switches separately.

Windows/macOS Tone Studio now imports speaker IRs without a separate loader application. iOS/Android wireless editing continues to require optional BT-DUAL. Full category subtotals, exact IR capacity and remaining synth/acoustic algorithms are not independently re-certified in this scoped pass.

## IR-200 — partial expression capability lead

### IR-200 — MIDI bridging and USB loopback mode qualification

Checked **2026-11-04**, [official owner manual eng02, printed pp19–20](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/IR-200_eng02_W.pdf). MIDI THRU and USB THRU each offer OFF/USB/MIDI/U+M: received messages can be forwarded to the selected connector(s). This establishes configurable MIDI bridging, not audio channel count or pedal wiring. The PC map independently maps each received program1–128 to memory1–128.

The USB table lists dry output, effect input/output, mix input and loopback controls under VENDOR; GENERIC lists effect output and mix input only. Roland’s [remote-session article, October7,2021](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/4407987892507-IR-200-Holding-an-online-session-remote-session-on-your-computer-with-sound-inputted-from-the-IR-200) describes mixing computer playback back into computer recording, gives both desktop Vendor and iOS Generic setup, then presents loopback instructions without a mode qualification. **Generic/iOS loopback availability remains unresolved**; do not generalize desktop controls from the article’s ordering. This is computer-audio loopback, not an onboard phrase looper. Numeric audio stream matrix, sample formats and expression attenuation remain unverified. No software installed or device tested; partial lead remains outside active inventory.

### IR-200 — English revision 02 is not firmware 1.10 documentation

Checked **2026-11-04** against the [official English owner manual eng02, ©2021, cover code022](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/IR-200_eng02_W.pdf), printed pp17–19. This additional accessible manufacturer revision supplements, rather than replaces, the retained eng01 source. Its expression table still distinguishes SYSTEM assignment preference, MIN/MAX endpoints and separate pedal-state retention. None establishes final-output coverage.

Crucially, p19 still describes AUTO OFF as OFF/ON with a ten-hour interval. The separately retained January2025 firmware1.10 notes specify OFF/20MIN/10HRS and default20MIN. Thus **eng02 is not evidence that these pages describe firmware1.10**, despite its higher document suffix. Publication date beyond copyright and exact firmware applicability remain unverified. Use the versioned release notes for changed power behavior; keep bypass, headphone, AUX/USB and ambience attenuation unresolved. No download or device operation; partial lead remains outside active inventory.

### IR-200 — pedal hold and USB routing boundaries

Checked 2026-10-19 against the [same manufacturer-authored manual, pp13–19](https://manuals.plus/m/b9825b3bebd56cb099901e5e15ff82bed29bc40deea57a2f0c884f79aaf4a363_optim.pdf). AMP LEVEL controls the entire preamp, not documented final output. **EXP PDL HOLD** separately determines whether operational state carries across memory changes; it does not establish assignment scope.

USB MIX IN joins at the final MAIN OUT stage in Vendor and Generic modes. Therefore AMP LEVEL must not be promised as an all-source mute; exact bypass/ambience/headphone attenuation remains unverified. Vendor mode is for Windows/macOS with driver; Generic uses standard iOS functions with reduced features. Vendor offers separate dry out, effects input/output and loopback controls; these are not a verified numerical USB channel count.

Send/Return can sit before cabinets or after EQ; NORMAL inserts external sound in series, DIR MIX combines return and direct sound. Send/return audio levels0–200 are distinct from expression MIN/MAX0–100. Headphone volume and ambience each have0–100 settings. No hardware, firmware or software operation.

### IR-200 — cabinet-library arithmetic and routing alternatives

Checked **2026-11-03** in the original [Sound List 02, ©2021, pp3–8](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/IR-200_SoundList_eng02_W.pdf), with the [current manufacturer product tables](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ir-200/) as corroboration. Text review only; no download or device operation.

- **BOSS library: 144 combinations = 12 cabinet families ×6 mic/mix options ×2 distances.** Eight guitar families supply96 combinations; four bass families supply48. The mic/mix options comprise five individual microphones and one equal-volume two-microphone blend. These are stored IR choices, not144 independently modeled speaker families or simultaneous blocks.
- **Celestion library: 10 source files**, including one stereo room/multi-mic file with separately listed left/right selections. Counting those channel rows yields11 selections, not11 independent source files. This distinguishes the published154-file library from a raw menu-row count. It does not resolve user stereo import/storage behavior.
- **Source conflict:** one Celestion entry’s cabinet name begins412 while its speaker column and source filename indicate212. The current product table also says2×12. Preserve the label discrepancy; do not infer another cabinet.
- **Routing alternatives:** p8 illustrates two cabinet-output modes ×four return modes ×two loop positions = **16 configurations**. They reuse cabinet A/B and EQ A/B; this is not16 simultaneous chains. Mono/stereo return and direct-mix choices remain distinct.

Current installed content, user stereo-IR handling, final-output expression coverage and full profile admission remain unresolved. The partial lead remains outside active counts.

### IR-200 — loaded assets versus saved assignments and headphone voicing

Checked **2026-11-03** against model-specific Roland support text. No software, firmware, files or device settings were changed.

| Field | Manufacturer evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| IR storage versus memory assignment | **October10,2025:** USED IN MEMORY=NO means the asset is already loaded but not written into a memory. Select its USER IR in the Cabinet setting and WRITE the destination memory; then the indicator becomes YES. Import alone does not assign that IR across presets. [Exact support article](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/40956771382683-IR-200-USED-IN-MEMORY-shows-NO-after-loading-a-custom-IR-in-the-IR-Loader) |
| Stereo capacity versus channel selection | [December23,2024 support answer](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/4407995602331-IR-200-Can-IR-data-created-on-third-party-devices-or-apps-be-loaded-into-the-IR-200) confirms **128 mono /64 stereo files**. [November27,2025 loading instructions](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/25853792937627-IR-200-How-do-I-load-IR-data) still require choosing one channel from stereo data. Both statements remain valid evidence; exact pairing/storage and automatic L/R assignment remain unverified. Do not downgrade capacity to mono-only or invent a tested workflow. |
| Headphones versus output voicing | [October7,2021 support answer](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/4407987847963-IR-200-Do-I-need-to-configure-OUTPUT-SELECT-even-if-I-m-just-monitoring-the-sound-via-headphones-or-earphones-connected-to-the-PHONES-OUT-jack): PHONES always uses line-output characteristics; OUTPUT SELECT configures the OUTPUT jacks. This is voicing scope, not headphone power, final volume or expression attenuation. |
| USB count wording | [IR-2/IR-200 comparison](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/19276168695451-IR-2-IR-200-Differences-between-the-IR-2-and-IR-200) lists IR-200 USB as **2 (Stereo×2)**. Retain that exact count basis; it does not independently specify record/playback directions, all driver modes or sample formats. The physical Micro-B connector remains one socket. |

Global AMP LEVEL output coverage, full-editor capability and exact USB stream matrix remain unresolved. Partial lead remains outside active inventory; this evidence does not displace the separate-display priority or the independent GP-200JR → Prime → GE300 cursor.

### IR-200 — electrical levels and headphone load

Checked **2026-11-03** against [BOSS’s exact-model specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ir-200/). Published ratings, not measurements; page does not identify a hardware/firmware revision.

| Field | Manufacturer specification / boundary |
|---|---|
| Input / return | **1 MΩ**; nominal **−10 dBu**, maximum **+7 dBu**. This supports high-impedance instrument input; it is not an EXP potentiometer rating. |
| Auxiliary input | **27 kΩ**; nominal **−20 dBu**, maximum **0 dBu**. |
| Output / send | **1 kΩ** output impedance; recommended load **≥10 kΩ**. Nominal **−10 dBu**, maximum **+7 dBu**. |
| Headphones | **44 Ω output impedance**, separately **≥44 Ω recommended headphone load**; nominal **−10 dBu**, maximum **+7 dBu**. No power-per-channel or distortion/load test conditions supplied here. |
| Conversion / processing | **96 kHz**, ADC **32-bit + AF method**, DAC **32-bit**, **32-bit floating-point processing**. These do not establish every USB stream format or IR source-file conversion rule. |

Headphone load, output impedance and gain limits do not prove expression attenuation of phones, AUX, USB or surviving ambience. Existing stereo-IR and global AMP LEVEL qualifications remain. Partial lead stays outside active counts; no download or hardware operation.

### KATANA:GO — direct analog expression screen

Checked 2026-10-28 against [own current manufacturer specifications][KTGO-P]. Ordinary guitar/bass input is documented. Published connectors are the instrument plug, stereo-mini PHONES and USB-C: **no direct analog EXP socket**. EV-1-WL or an expression pedal via FS-1-WL is an indirect wireless/MIDI route, not an analog pedal connection to this processor. Global analog-volume assignment, placement/endpoints/recall are therefore inapplicable to the requested path.

Current body **45W×125Dmm** fits preference; preserve earlier physical-design and seller-GO2 qualifications. Dedicated headphones are documented; **no onboard footswitches** in the control inventory. A/B/C and multifunction buttons are hand controls. Wireless-volume bypass, headphone, Bluetooth/USB backing-audio, tail and recorded-loop coverage remain unverified. A physical master control does not establish equivalent wireless target coverage. No controller, software or hardware operation.

### ME-5 — indexed memory and modulation evidence

Checked2026-10-17 against targeted indexed passages of [official original manual][ME5-M],printed pp5,33. Direct PDF text remains unavailable;no visual inspection.

| Field | Verified evidence / limits |
|---|---|
| Factory / user memory | **64 manufacturer-programmed,rewritable locations** arranged4groups×4banks×4numbers. Creating a sound starts from a patch that may be erased;write is required. Unsaved edits are lost on power-off or selecting another operating mode. No separate64-slot factory bank established. |
| Processing / chorus-flanger | Five alternatives in one block:mode1 chorus;modes2–5 flanger. Higher flanger-mode numbers move processing toward higher frequency bands. Not five simultaneous modulation blocks. |

The same manual's indexed outline describes five effect units and their stored on/off states;an explicit maximum simultaneous statement still needs corroboration. Overdrive-mode details remain pending;ME-6B search results are not ME-5 evidence.

### ME-5 — simultaneous units and MIDI memory addressing

Checked2026-10-17 against indexed original [official manual][ME5-M]:“Operation/ModeSelection”,“PatchEditing”,and“MIDI Implementation Version1.00”. Search excerpts only;no visualinspection.

| Field | Verified behavior / limits |
|---|---|
| Processing / simultaneous | **All five units can be on together**:Compressor,Overdrive/Distortion,EQ,Chorus/Flanger,Reverb/Delay. Shared alternatives remain shared;noise suppression/external loop are additional functions. Manual cautions high-gain combinations may generate noise/oscillation. |
| Processing / drive | MIDI parameter table defines **three modes,1–3**,encoded0–2. Individual mode descriptions remain unverified;do not substitute ME-6B labels. |
| Controls / mechanism | ModeSelect is a push button cyclingPlay→Edit→MIDI;Manual entered separately. PatchEditing describes display/parameter/value editing instead of physical parameter knobs. Supports text-derived0rotary/5foot/8buttons inventory;power separate. |
| MIDI / memory | Transmit programs1–64;receive1–128,with65–128 mapped back to1–64. Transmit/receive channel cannot differ. SysEx bulk all/current andparameter communication documented;these are preset/parameter data,not evidence of audio/IR/NAM import. |

| Processing / categories | Compressor; drive; Humanizer/EQ; Harmonist/Pitch; Chorus/Flanger/Phaser/Tremolo; delay; reverb; suppression; amp simulator. **23 effects available; up to 11 simultaneously**. Choice counts and exclusions remain partly unresolved. | [Manual pp3,34][ME8-M] |
| Categories / drive and harmonies | **Five analog drive alternatives**: Overdrive, Turbo Overdrive, Blues Driver, Distortion, Hard Distortion (Metal Zone); External selects a connected drive. Harmonist adds **two harmony notes** to the guitar, producing three-note harmony. | [Manual pp3,20][ME8-M] |
| Live / control and transitions | CTL function is assignable per patch. Manual pedals switch individual effects; tempo pedals set delay time/rate. Delay/reverb tails continue when moving to a patch without those effects. Exact transition restrictions pending. | [Manual p3][ME8-M] |
| Processing / Humanizer or EQ | **Mutually exclusive** Humanizer and three-band EQ. Humanizer alternates two vowels selected independently from u/o/i/e/a; trigger can restart from vowel 1 on a played string. EQ has parametric mids (63 Hz–11 kHz) and separate Presence. Level ranges −18…18 are displayed parameter values; dB units not established by this passage. | [Manual p22][ME8-M] |
| Processing / harmony or pitch | Harmonist and Pitch Shifter are alternatives. Harmonist requires **single-note playing**, with two harmony levels, direct level and major/minor song key. Pitch Shifter supports chords; two shifted voices each cover **−12…+12 semitones**, with independent Fine −20…20 (units unestablished here). | [Manual pp23–24][ME8-M] |
| Categories / shared modulation | **Nine alternatives**: Chorus, Stereo Chorus, Flanger, Hi Band Flanger, Phaser 1–4, Tremolo. Phaser 1/2/3 use four/eight/ten phases; Phaser 4 chains two phasers. Indexed MODE summary repeats PH2 where the detailed list identifies PH3; preserve that transcription discrepancy. | [Manual p24][ME8-M] |
| Categories / reverb | **Six alternatives**: Hall 1/2, Room 1/2, Plate 1/2. TIME **1–20 is a displayed parameter range**, with seconds not established by the passage; Tone −50…50 and effect level 0…100. | [Manual p27][ME8-M] |
| Expression / control-effect restrictions | **Slow Attack is CTL-only; Foot Volume is EXP-only**. Foot Volume preserves delay/reverb decay when lowering the pre-ambience signal. Feedbacker requires a stable single note; CTL press raises feedback and release mutes it, while EXP varies its level. Intelligent Ring Modulator recommends single notes and remains ring-modulated whenever selected. Pedal Wah supports CTL or continuous EXP. | [Manual p13][ME8-M] |
| Categories / control targets | Assignments include Pedal Wah, Feedbacker, Ring Modulator, Intelligent Ring Modulator, Slow Attack, Foot Volume, Arm Down 1–3, Arm Up 1–2, Trip 1–2, Vibrato, Silent, Tempo, OD/DS, HR/PS, modulation, Delay, Reverb and Tuner; Off additional. Arm Down 1 reaches two octaves down. These are assignable choices, not additional simultaneous blocks. | [Manual p13][ME8-M] |
| Expression / Arm, Trip and Vibrato | Arm Down 2 lowers two semitones; Down 3 one octave; Up 1 raises three semitones; Up 2 one octave. Trip 1 adds a voice up to two octaves above dry; Trip 2 adds one up to an octave below dry. CTL drives timed pitch movement; EXP follows pedal position. Vibrato: CTL gradually applies it while held; EXP varies depth and lifted position disables it. | [Manual p14][ME8-M] |
| Live / Silent, Tempo and remote restrictions | **CTL-only**: Silent mutes all outputs and clears audible delay/reverb tails; Tempo requires more than four presses; Remote toggles drive, harmony/pitch, modulation, delay, reverb or tuner. These targets do not work with EXP. Silent is distinct from Foot Volume, which preserves ambience decay. | [Manual p14][ME8-M] |
| Categories / amp simulator and suppression | Amp simulator selects **Small Box or Large Box**, with Off additional. Noise-suppression threshold spans Off–100; excessive threshold can suppress quiet playing. Master Level 80 gives equal input/output level when all effects are off. CTL and optional EXP have separate assignment parameters. | [Manual p28][ME8-M] |
| Preset memory | **50 user + 50 preset = 100**, plus Manual settings;user locations initially duplicate presets,as clarified below. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | Dedicated synthesis/acoustic simulation unverified. | Unresolved |
| I/O | Input; L/Mono and R outputs; stereo mini headphones; external drive send/return; Manual Remote; Tuner Remote; EXP; Tempo In; adapter. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |
| Power / battery / USB | **14 V AC; 500 mA draw**. Battery/USB operation unverified; AC has no fixed DC center polarity. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |

### ME-8 — tempo-delay ceiling and shared tempo

Checked2026-10-18 against manufacturer-indexed [own manual p16][ME8-M], How to Set the Patch for Using the Tempo Delay/Tempo Rate. No PDF downloaded or device operation performed.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Delay / tempo ceiling | Tempo Delay supports intervals **t-1–t-9**, with **2.0seconds maximum**. When basic tempo plus interval would exceed that maximum, the actual delay time is **halved**. Do not assume the selected subdivision is preserved beyond the ceiling. |
| Live / simultaneous tempo functions | Tempo Delay and Tempo Rate can operate together in a patch. Humanizer,Flanger,Phaser andTremolo offer tempo-rate settings;Flanger/Phaser also offer tempo-step settings. These parameter options are not extra effect blocks. |
| Live / basic tempo persistence | Optional **FS-5U at TEMPO IN** or CTL assigned TEMPO sets tempo after **more than four steady presses**. Basic tempo starts at **quarter-note120** at each power-on and retains changes until power-off;it can be changed even while the current patch does not use it. No patch-stored tempo or MIDI clock support is inferred. |


### ME-8 — delay choices, compressor and initialization

Checked2026-10-18 against manufacturer-indexed own-manual pp20,26 and Roland support. Documentation only; no reset, device test or source-file download.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Category choices / delay2 | **Mono and Tap** are the two delay types. TIME spans **1ms–2.00s**;Tap is intended for stereo output and its actual delay time is **half the set value**. This mode behavior is separate from the tempo-ceiling rule above. With feedback0,Mono produces one repeat;Tap produces one delayed sound on each side. [Own manual p26][ME8-M] |
| Category choices / compressor | One described compressor has Sustain/Attack/Level0–100 andTone−50–50. LowSustain behaves like limiting;this is a parameter behavior,not a separately selectable limiter model. Displayed values do not establish milliseconds orcompression-ratio units. [Own manual p20][ME8-M] |
| Memory / factory reset scope | Roland warns that initialization loses **all user information**. Its documented PARAMETER-left/down power-on andWRITE/COPY confirmation restores factory state;it is not a settings-only reset orproof of an external backup editor. [Manufacturer support](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201957579-ME-8-Initialization-Factory-Reset) |

Checked2026-10-18. [Roland’s model-specific specification](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201925899-ME-8-Technical-Specifcations),updated2026-10-02, and manufacturer-indexed own-manual p17. No hardware measurements or device operation.

|---|---|

This describes internal conversion,not USB audio or a claim of20-bit ADC resolution. [Roland specification](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201925899-ME-8-Technical-Specifcations) |

Retain the manufacturer’s unit convention;these are nominal levels,not maximum headroom orproof of balanced output. Optional FS-5U at MANUAL REMOTE toggles Manual/Play. This differs from immediate continuous autosave. [Own manual p17][ME8-M] |

### ME-8B — original-manual synth architecture

The [manufacturer-authored Spanish manual copy](https://www.casaveerkamp.net/archivos/ME-8B.pdf), ©1996 BOSS,38PDFpages,labels the relevant section as English-manual p24 (PDF27). **WAVE1–4** use an internal source following bass pitch/attack: saw orsquare,each with an octave-down variant. The manual explicitly requires single notes,muting other strings andthe preceding note,with a clear attack. **WAVE5–9** directly reshape the incoming waveform;their chord handling is unverified. **WAVE10** is volume-responsive touch wah,not a separate tracking synth. This readable original-body mirror supplements the unreadable primary English scan;translation/revision equivalence andinstalled firmware remain unverified.

### ME-8B — controls and Manual-mode memory limits

The same [©1996 original-body manual copy](https://www.casaveerkamp.net/archivos/ME-8B.pdf), English references6,8,18–20, identifies **seven footswitches**: numbered1–4,Bank,Control andBypass. It names six function buttons—Tuner,Write/Copy,Manual,Group,Escape,Edit—plus Value/Pitch andParameter controls. Missing diagrams prevent a complete button/rotary/treadle census;these counts stay separate.

**Manual-mode edits save on return to Play**,without Write;powering off first loses them. Compressor Enhancer/Defretter andReverb use parameter-based switching rather than ordinary stomp toggles. **Bypass still permits patch changes**;this does not establish true bypass orvolume-pedal coverage. **Tuner mutes output andprevents patch selection**;reference pitch435–445Hz in1Hz steps survives power-off. No reset ordevice operation was performed.

Continuation: retain this model as a partial lead;unresolved diagram-dependent controls,full effect-choice census andqualified Indonesian offers stay queued. Resume individual-LCD processor priorities rather than repeatedly reopening unchanged market/access gaps.

No local download,dependency/software installation ordevice operation. Next: inspect originalmanual sections andfind exactIndonesian hardware offers before considering active admission;independent LCD/expression/general queues remain unchanged.

| External control / cable | Two FS-5Us require a stereo send/return insert cable, per Roland’s 2025-10-08 ME-50/ME-50B article. This establishes switching cabling, not external expression support. | [Roland cable support][M50B-C] |
| Expression volume limits | Volume-pedal minimum/maximum levels cannot be configured. Roland suggests a louder stored patch for solo boosts. | [Japanese manufacturer FAQ][M50B-E] |
| Tuner / live source conflict | English support says Tuner automatically mutes; manual p25 and Japanese FAQ describe audible dry bypass with pedal-volume muting. Preserve the contradiction; no tested resolution. Japanese FAQ also qualifies a WAH-mode exception. | [English tuner support][M50B-TE]; [Japanese tuner FAQ][M50B-TJ]; [Manual p25][M50B-M] |
| Analog levels / conversion | INPUT/AUX nominal −10dBu; impedance 1Mohm/100kohm respectively. Output −10dBu,2kohm. A/D 24-bit+AF; D/A 24-bit;44.1kHz. | [Product][M50B-P] |
